# Homilies IX–XII (Omelie IX-XII)

**Author**: Sévère Ibn-al-Moqaffa`  
**Series**: PO Tome 59  
**Columns**: pp. 454–618  

[p. 454] {#po.59.p454}

### HOMILY IX
ድርሳን ፡ ታስዕ ።
Introduction to the theme and ritual greeting
ድርሳን ፡ ታስዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከሚት'፤ ፡ ምንት" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሞት ፡ ዘሞኦ' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዲበ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወያበጥል ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሥሩዕ ፡ ያሙቶሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ኦእጥየ፤ ፣ ፍቁር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያብርህ" ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልብከ' ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ ህ መንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፣ ከመ ፣ ትለቡ ፣ ምሥጢራተ ፣ መለኮቱ ።
1 Ninth homily, illustration concerning the death that God defeated on the cross and refuting the discourse of those who maintain that it would be an angel on God's part appointed to kill humans. O my beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your mind with the light of his Holy Spirit the Comforter so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity.

me you create man who sins, fornicates, steals, kills, and makes God angry”. The angel feared the oath, left the mud, returned to God, and told him this. He sent an angel again, and the mud did the same thing with him, so much so that he sent six angels and the mud made them swear. They feared the oath and left it. The angel Muryal succeeds in convincing the mud 4 When he sent the seventh (angel), by the name of Muryal according to their description, so that he might take the mud, he made the mud swear. He did not fear the oath but took the mud and brought it before God. God said to him: “Since you have listened to me, you have not opposed me, you have not feared the oath that the mud made you swear, I give you power over him to kill him until eternity”. God instantly changed the handsome and noble appearance of that angel, and his appearance became grim and terrifying. Creation of man from mud

5 At the same instant, from the mud he also made the image of the human body and said: “Who will become a guarantor for this man regarding the sins he commits, until I create a soul inside him?”. That remained so, for forty days, mud without a soul, until someone arrived who would act as his guarantor. When he created him, he ordered all the angels to prostrate themselves before him, and all prostrated themselves except one of them who disobeyed and did not prostrate himself before Adam, and he sentenced his fall: this is Satan. He left instead

Adam in paradise for one hundred years, after which he created Eve from him. Adam and Eve lived another hundred years, after which they transgressed and fell.

### Reply against the lying legend

6 And you say that such a homily is a witness to this angel and his investiture to cause men to die. The fallacy of this homily, O my beloved, is evident to all those who know the book of God, and to him who has intelligence. Indeed, that mendacious homily is clearly for the foolish and even more so for the intelligent. He who believes this homily is devoid of intelligence, sense, and knowledge. God does not need to summon the mud. The Bible refutes the legend 10 7 The beginning of its fallacy and its lie is the assertion that God sent his angels one after the other so that they might bring him mud from which he would create men. Thus, he has circumscribed God to a place and constrained him to send him who had to bring him the mud. He also says: “The mud spoke and made the angel swear that he would not bring it before God to create from it the man who kills, fornicates, and makes God angry”. In this way, he has made the mud better

than God and the angels, as if it knew the mysteries, spoke, and prophesied.

8 But it is known that mud is deaf, arid, devoid of intelligence and mobility. This is a lie, and the composer of this homily who remembered him who brought the mud before God; the mud is one of the four natural elements from which (God) created man. Why then did he not remember the other natural elements, fire, water, air? How did their arrival (before God) happen? He should have explained it, just as he remembered the arrival of the mud. It is clearly evident that it is a mendacious homily because the book of creation that Moses wrote on behalf of God says that God took the mud from the earth (Gen 2:7) and does not say: “He sent him who would bring him (the mud) and from it created man”. On the creation of humans

9 By “man” is meant a perfect man, flesh, bones, blood, soul, mind, and speech. It is not, therefore, as that mendacious homily recounts that (God) created mud without a soul, but he created him perfect, and immediately breathed into him the spirit of life (Gen 2:7), that is, the grace of the Holy Spirit through which he was united with God and his soul became alive in God. That grace abandoned him when he transgressed. Thus says the book of God that on that day in which he created him, he made the numbness of sleep descend upon him and from him created Eve (Gen 2:21-22).

10 And when they transgressed, on that day he made them fall, before the evening of that day. It did not happen as the composer of that homily lies, maintaining that he (Adam) lived one hundred years in paradise before (God) created Eve from him, and that after having created her (they lived) one hundred years, before they transgressed. As for what he asserts about the prostration of the angels to Adam, this has never been pleasing to the faithful and they have never expressed it because it would have been an insult toward God and toward the angels, if they had made (the angels) prostrate themselves before creatures, before the Creator.

### The disobedience of Satan and his fall

declared that Satan fell on the day of Sunday on which he was created, six days before Adam was created. The reason for his fall was his refusal of the praise of God together with the angels, in pride, and he presented himself as god. Thus says the prophet Isaiah about him: “How did the morning star fall that was higher than all the stars?” (Is 14:12). He, in fact, boasted and said: “I will establish my seat above the clouds and I will make myself like the Most High” (2 Enoch 29:4).

12 The prophet has therefore declared that the reason for his fall is his arrogance, and nothing else of what the composer of this mendacious homily has said. If it had been as the composer of that homily says, that Adam was created before the fall of Satan, why (then) did God decide to create Adam? Rather, truthful is the word that the sacred books of God have manifested to us, that when Satan fell from the degree of his investiture, he and all the army of which he was the leader and which obeyed him and which together with him refused the praise, for this reason God created Adam in his stead, so that from Adam he could create a number corresponding to those who fell together with the devil, to then elevate them to the degree of investiture of the angels, from which the devil and his army fell. The reason for the devil's envy

13 His (lost) position is the reason for which the devil had envy of them; he plotted against them, until they became proud like him. They wanted to become gods (Gen 3:5) but God made them fall like him. God condemned them, and the condemnation against him is eternal because his guilt is equal to their guilt. So much so that the Son of God offered himself for them and took upon himself the condemnation they deserved, he purchased them with his blood (Eph 1:7; 1 Pt 1:19) and restored them to the position for which they were created (2 Cor 5:17). If it had been as the composer of that mendacious homily maintains, both the creation and the fall of Adam would have happened without guilt, and the humanization of the Son of God, his crucifixion, and everything he accomplished would have been in vain.

### Again on Muryal, the angel of death

14 The composer of that mendacious homily maintains that God, to him whom he had named Muryal, gave the power to kill humans because (Muryal) had had the audacity to bring him the mud. He changed his handsome and joyful appearance; he became evil, and he who terrifies humans and kills them. And why ever did God create him for whom he had created death even before creating him? They have rendered vain the books that maintain that God created humans for life (Wis 2:23-24). How then did he create him for life and create for him him who kills and destroys him? O beloved! I have declared all this to you (to point out) that that homily is mendacious. They attributed it to the holy father Theophilus, as I have already reminded you. Saint John, bishop of the city of Boralas (Parallos), expelled many mendacious homilies; he expelled them from the holy monasteries and burned them with fire. Absurdity of the legend about the angel of death

15 An angel cannot cause all humans to die; humans do not die in a single place, nor inside a single house. Indeed, at the same hour, many people die in all the extremities of the earth. If it is a single angel who causes everyone to die, he is God and not an angel, because such an angel is extended to all the extremities of the earth. However, there does not exist any angel extended to all the extremities of the earth, nor any who fills the whole earth except God, who alone fills every place and no place circumscribes him and there is no place that is without him. On the contrary, all the angels are limited, circumscribed, and move from one place to another. If one of them arrives in a place, the place (he left) will remain without him.

16 It is extraordinary, this, that an angel with a black face appears in all the extremities of the earth. If, by chance, he had been luminous, due to the extension of his light, it would have been possible for him (to shine) like the sun and the moon. To declare that the holy angel of God is an angel of death, this is bad. How can the angel of life be an angel who causes death?

### Paul the apostle on the origin of death
ወአኮ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሞት ፣ ወኢበፈቃዱ ። አላ ፣ ሞትሰ ፡፣ መጽአ" ፡ እምኃጢአት ። ወኃጢአት" ፡ እምከይሲ ፡ ዘውአእቱ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ወአኮ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፣ እምዲያብሎስ'' ፡ መጽአ ፣ ሞት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወበሞት'' ፡ ሲኦል ። ወ፪ ፡ ሞትሰ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሃ ፎሐ እምዲያብሎስ ፡ ሞተ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስመ ፡ ምክንያተ፡፪ ፣ እምኃጢአተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወምክነያተ ፡ ኃጢአትሰ'"' ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ከይሲ!" ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡፣ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ቦአት" ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጵ ብእሲ ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሞት ። ወበእ ንተዝ ፣ ነግሠ ፣ ሞት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ። ናሁ ፡ አጠየቀ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እስመ ፣ እምኃጢአት ፣ ነግሠ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወኃጢአት ፡ ወሞት ፡ እሙንቱ ፣
17 Death did not come from God, nor by his will; rather, death came from sin (Rom 5:12-19), and sin came from the serpent, who is the devil (Gen 3:1-19; Wis 2:24), and not from God. Death supervened from the devil because of sin, and through death, Sheol. The two deaths, the death of the body and the death of Sheol, are from the devil. Indeed, the cause of both (deaths) is sin, and the cause of sin is the devil, that is, the serpent. As the apostle Paul says: “Death entered the world because of one man, and through sin came death, and thus death reigned over every man” (Rom 5:12, 14). The apostle has therefore clarified that death reigned through sin and that sin and death are from Satan. 15 Jesus declares Satan the “origin of death”.

18 As our Lord Jesus Christ said to the Jews about him in the Gospel of John: “You seek to kill me, as Satan your father did, because he is a slayer of the souls of humans from the beginning” (Jn 8:40, 44). Our Lord Jesus Christ has therefore manifested that Satan is a slayer of men from the beginning, and he did not say that he is a slayer of souls in Sheol, but rather a slayer of bodies. He said: “You seek to kill me, just as your father kills humans.” We know that the Jews killed our Lord Jesus Christ only with the killing of the body. Jesus and Paul on Satan.

Sheol. The death that reigned by reason of it (sin), this is the death of the body, because the death of the body is the descent of the soul to Sheol. If death reigns through sin, sin is undoubtedly from Satan, and Satan is the authority of death and sin because they are in his hand and emanated from him. 5

### The testimony of Saint Basil

20 Saint Basil in his Eucharistic liturgy, in the prayer of peace, says: “Great, eternal God who created man without corruption, and the death that entered the world through the envy of the devil (Wis 2:24), you destroyed it with the life-giving manifestation of your only-begotten Son, our Lord and our God, and our Savior Jesus Christ.” Observe, O beloved, the word of this saint, that God created humans without corruption. Why, then, does the composer of that homily maintain that God, before creating him, appointed over him the one who would kill him and cause him to rot? Behold, Basil manifested that when he created him without corruption, death came upon him from the devil and not from God. Likewise, in the Eucharistic prayer, he says again that our Lord Jesus Christ became incarnate and became human and offered himself to the death that reigned over us through sin, for our ransom. Analysis of Saint Basil’s data. Death is from the devil.

21 Did our Lord Jesus Christ give himself for us to an angel who obeys him, and did he ransom us from one of his angels? Was it perhaps an angel who reigned over us through sin? Behold, therefore, it has been revealed to us that the one who reigned over us through sin is the devil, who reigned over us through our obedience to him. And the Son of God offered himself until he redeemed us. He indeed called him to account with his blood and killed him when he offered himself and took us from his hand and ransomed us with his blood, and took us for his indemnity. As for death, it is from the devil and his impure army and not from God, nor from his pure angels. If it had been a holy angel appointed by God to make humans die, for what reason was Christ crucified and did he die? He is indeed capable of rescuing us from his angel who obeys him, without having to die for our sake and ransom us with his life.

22 But the prophet was a witness that death fought against Christ and that Christ defeated it, for he says: “Death was swallowed up in victory! Where, O death, is your sting? Where, O Sheol, is your power?” (Is 25:8; Hos 13:14 // 1 Cor 15:54c-55). Does an angel commanded by God truly fight the Son of God? And does the prophet truly declare about an angel obedient to God that he was swallowed up by victory? Did the prophet perhaps rejoice at the destruction of God’s angel? And he said: “Where, O death, is your sting?” (Is 25:8; Hos 13:14 // 1 Cor 15:54c-55). Behold, therefore, it is manifested here that the death of the body and the death of Sheol derive from the enemy of God. For this reason, the prophet of God rejoiced at his destruction when he says: “Where, O death, is your sting? Where, O Sheol, is your power?” (Is 25:8; Hos 13:14 // 1 Cor 15:54c-55). Modes by which the devil kills humans.

23 By “sting” he means his evil appearance with which he strikes the hearts of bodies, just as a thorn strikes, and terrifies them to such an extent that their blood dries up and they die by the death of their blood. Indeed, the soul of every living being is its blood, as God said to Noah in the book of creation: “When their blood dries up, their rational and speech-endowed souls will no longer reside in their body” (Gen 9:4-5). Indeed, bodies are thick while spirits are subtle. God, the creator, joined the subtle spirits with the thick (material) bodies, establishing blood in the midst of them, because the subtlety of blood is like a relative of the spirit, while the density (thickness/materiality) is like a relative of the body. The encounter with Satan at the moment of death. On the fall of Satan and the enmity with humans. 24 When the person is terrified by the evil appearance of the angels of death, that is, the army of Satan that appears to him on the day of his death, his thirst increases from his great terror, his blood dries up in his belly. And when his blood dries up, the spirit does not remain in the thick (material) body. If the soul separates from the body, it is taken back by the angel appointed

during his life, either by God or by Satan. Indeed, when at the beginning God created the angels when he created heaven and earth, the head of the primordial exalted order was Satan; he became proud, and they fell from the exalted order, he and all his army that was with him. God created Adam and Eve to complete through them, and from those who would be created from them, the exalted order from which the devil fell.

### Mechanism of the transmission of original sin

25 Satan knew this and ensnared them until they sinned, (exactly) like him, and were subjected to his power. They fell from paradise to the earth on which he (Satan) had fallen. Then he (Satan) appointed over him and over Eve two (soldiers) from his army, and impure spirits and angels of death who had fallen together with him. When they coupled like beasts and begat children, he appointed over each of his children an impure spirit; since the fathers became his servants, in the same way their children became his servants. Humans subjugated by Satan from conception until death. The birth of Christ changes the fate of humans.

26 And it happened that that impure spirit, together with the man, was with him forever, until the day in which God decides his death and that impure spirit with the evil appearance that is with him manifests himself to him (at the point of death). At that moment his terror increases, his thirst grows, his blood dries up, his soul separates from his body. The impure spirit makes it descend into Sheol, which is in the depths of the earth. Thus humans lived for 5,500 years until Christ, the Son of God, was born of the pure Virgin Mary, without human seed. And when he was born, it was not possible for any of the impure spirits, according to their habit of settling upon the newborn, to approach the door of the

27 They hastened (to go) to their leader, Satan, the liar (Jn 8:44) enemy, and told him this. The lying enemy feared the prophecies of the prophets who foretold this and said that the Son of God would come and would save Adam and his lineage from him (Satan) (Gen 3:15). When his army reported to him about the great number of the army of angels that surrounded the cave in which he was born, he feared and knew that the newborn was the one whom the prophets had prophesied. Satan goes to the cave but the newborn hides his identity.

28 He hastened (to go) personally, recognized, and arrived at the cave. And our Lord, who came to arrange the mystery of our salvation, wanted to hide himself from him so that he would not know that he was the Son of God. He made a sign to all the angels to ascend into the heavens and in such a way shook the heart of his mother. She wrapped him in cloths and laid him in the manger, and when Satan arrived at the cave and did not see any of the army of angels that his army had seen, but instead saw our Lord in that miserable condition, he immediately had doubts about him and became convinced that he was not God but a weak man; he abandoned him and went away.

### Flight into Egypt

29 He then incited King Herod to kill him to have relief from fearing him. Joseph, the Virgin Mary, and our Lord fled immediately to the land of Egypt; the fear of Satan diminished and he became certain that he was a weak man, because if he had been God he would not have fled. And thus our Lord behaved with him for all the thirty years that he lived on earth; before his baptism he did not reveal to him his power nor his authority so that he would not recognize him.

Satan recognizes Jesus during his baptism. Jesus in the desert: confusion of Satan. 30 When, however, he was baptized by John in the Jordan River and John was his witness that he was the Son of God (Jn 1:34), he saw the Holy Spirit as he descended from the heavens upon him in the form of a dove and heard the voice of the Father saying: “You are my son whom I love and in you I am well pleased” (Mk 1:10-11 // Lk 3:22). Satan immediately realized that that was the Son of God and feared him. Our Lord knew this immediately and hastened (to go) to the desert and fasted forty days and forty nights. When Satan saw him fasting and praying, he doubted him and said: “If this were God he would not have needed fasting, nor prayer, but God called him his son, just as he called Israel ‘his son’ (Ex 4:22) even though he was not his son by his nature.” Satan incites the chief priests against Jesus to have him condemned to death.

31 Thus our Lord behaved with him in the thirty years in which he manifested his glory after his baptism. When he performed miracles by which his power was recognized, immediately after he operated in weakness to hide himself from Satan. By reason of his great doubting about him, and his turmoil over his actions, Satan agitated the heart of the chief priests of the Jews so that they would kill him, so that he would make him descend into Sheol as he had done against all the children of Adam and have relief from him.

### Satan goes into action during the Passion of Jesus
ሃ ወሶበ ፡ ቀሠፍዎ ፡ ወሰቀልዎ'"፤ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ቅንው ፡ እደ ዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ግብር ፣፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ድኩም ፡፣ ወአእመረ ፡፣ እምሠራዊቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፤አልቦ ፡ ወኢመኑሂ” ፡ ዘይቀትሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኢይክል” ፣ መኑሂ ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ" ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ በከመ ፣ ኮኑ ፡ የዐቅቡ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እለ ፡ ተወልዱ” ፡ እምደቂቁ” ፡ ለአዳም ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በርእሱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተ ርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በራእዩ” ፣ ዘሕሥሙም” ፡ ወመፍርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያደንግፆ ፡ ወያየ ብሶ” ፣ ደሞ ፡ በብዝኀ ፣ ፍርሀት ፣ ወጽምእ ፡ ወይሙት ።
32 When they scourged him and crucified him on the wood while his hands and his feet were nailed, Satan saw him in that condition and became certain that he was a weak man. He knew that none of his army would kill him, since none of his (Satan’s) soldiers had had the prerogative to settle inside him on the day of his birth, according to their habit of settling inside each newborn among the children of Adam; therefore Satan came to him in person to manifest himself to him with his evil and frightening appearance to terrify him and dry up his blood with such fear and thirst until he died.

33 When he arrived and stood in front of the cross of our Lord at the ninth hour of the day of Friday, as our Lord said to his disciples on the night of Friday that “the prince of this world will come to me, but he has no power over me” (Jn 14:30), this means: “that one cannot kill me,” but “so that the world may know that I love the Father. And as the Father told me, so I do” (Jn 14:31). This means that I offer myself of my own will (Jn 10:18) for the love of my Father to whom it pleased to offer me for the salvation of the world from his (the devil’s) hands.

### Face to face with Satan. Jesus’ strategy to capture Satan
ወሶበ ፣ አስተርአዮ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ በሕሱም ፡ ራእዩ ። ወርእዮ" ፡ ለእግዚእነ" ፡ ዘኢፈርሀ'' ፡ እምኔሁ፤' ፡ ወኢየብሰ ፣ ደሙ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ከመ! ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ኪነ ፣ "012ሀ.- ጥበቡ!' ፣ ዐቢይ ፡ ለእግዚእነ" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ በጥበቡ'"፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተኀበል ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ጥበበ! ፣ ዘሠርዐ'' ፡ ለአድኅኖተ ፡፣ ዓለም ። ወከልሐ ፡ ባበዐቢይ ፡ ቃል" ፣፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ነጽረኒ ፡ ወለምንት ፡ ኀደገኒ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ'" ፡ ሰይጣን፡! ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወኖናፈቀ፤! ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወኅለየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በድፍረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሃ 14:2ር ውእቱ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ኑፋቄ ፡ ወጥያቄ” ። ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ያሰስል ፡ ኑፋቄሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅረብ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍርሀት ፣ ይቤ” ፡ ጸማእኩ ።
34 When Satan manifested himself to our Lord with his evil appearance and saw that our Lord did not fear him, nor did his blood dry up, he became certain that he was the Son of God and immediately fled from him. This is the great wisdom of our Lord Jesus Christ. When he saw him, he fled from him. In his wisdom, he wanted him to dare against him so that he would bring to completion the wisdom that he had arranged for the salvation of the world. He cried out with a loud voice saying: “My God, my God, look at me! And why have you abandoned me?” (Mt 27:46 // Mk 15:34). When Satan heard him pronounce this word, he doubted him and thought that he was a weak man. He returned therefore to him with boldness, while being between doubt and certainty. Our Lord wanted to remove his doubt so that he would approach him without fear, therefore he said: “I thirst” (Jn 19:28). Satan in a trap.
ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፣ ኅለየ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የብእሲ ፡ ድኩም” ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ፈርሀ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ” ፡ ይቤ” ፣ ጸማእኩ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኑፋቄ ፣ በድፍረት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፣ ኀቤሁ” ፣ አፍለጠ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ነፍሰ ፡፣ ትስብእቱ ፣ እምሥጋሁ” ፣ በፈቃዱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይየብስ"' ፡ ደሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ"! ፡ በወንጌል" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አነ ፡ አነብራ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ ከመ ፣ አንሣአ” ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ወአልቦ ፣ ዘይነሥአ ፡ እም ኔየ ፡ አላ ፡ አነ ፡ ፣አነብራ ፡ በፈቃድየ” ፡ ወብየ ፡ ሥልጣነ" ፡ አነብራ ፡ ወብየ ፣
35 When Satan heard this word he thought that he was a weak man, and he feared him when he said “I thirst” (Jn 19:28). Then he approached him without (any) doubt with boldness. When (Satan) arrived at him, our Lord separated the soul of his humanity from his body, of his own will, without his blood drying up, as he says in the holy Gospel: “I lay down (at disposal) my soul in order to take it back, and there is no one who can take it from me; rather I lay it down (at disposal) of my own will and I have the power to lay it down (at disposal) and I have the power to take it back” (Jn 10:17-18).

And he laid down (at disposal) his soul and his body without his blood drying up, until he brought to completion the pretext against the enemy because he killed him.

### Capture of Satan and liberation of Adam in justice
ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተኀሥቻ ፣ በፍዳሁ' ፣ ወአሰሮ ፡፣ ወአውረዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መት ዢ ሕተ ፣ ሲኦል ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሱር ፡ በመአስር ፡ ዘኢይትፈታሕ ። ወቄወዎ' ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለዘርኡ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በተአዝዞቶሙ'' ፡ 612ህኋፌ ሎቱ ፣ ወአዕረጎሙ ፣ እመዋቅሒሁ ፣ ወነሥኦሙ ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ እምኔሁ'' ፡ ህየ ንተ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ቅትለቱ ፡ ወጥያቄ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይየብስ'' ፡ ደሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ረገዝዎ ፣ ገቦሁ ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ"' ፡ በሰዓት ፡ ወመንፈቅ ፡ ውኅዘ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ ደም ፡ ወማይ ።
36 Immediately (the Lord) called him to account for his indemnity/the price of the ransom. He chained him and made him descend into the depths of Sheol, chained, with bonds that do not loosen. He captured Adam and his lineage over whom the devil had reigned because they had submitted to him; he made them emerge from his chains (Eph 4:8; 1 Pt 3:19); he took them from him with justice as a price of ransom for his killing, and the certainty of his death without his blood drying up is that when an hour and a half after his death they struck his side, blood and water flowed from him (Jn 19:24). Jesus rises from the dead and appears to his disciples. Instruction on the administration

of baptism. 37 Then, on the third day, he brought his soul back to his body and rose from the dead. He manifested himself to his disciples and taught them the order of his baptism by means of which he saves the one who believes in him (Mk 16:16), among those who are under the power of Satan, the impure spirits and the angels of death who ensnare all those who are born on the earth so that they become their possession. In the same way, he instructed them so that whoever desires the faith should be brought to the holy Church and they should ask the Father through the Son so that he expels from him the impure spirit that was appointed in him from the day of his birth. Then he should turn his face toward the west and renounce Satan and all his army. The impure spirit that was with him from the devil will go out of him. And again he should turn his face toward the east and believe in Christ and they should ask the Father through the Son so that he makes the Holy Spirit descend upon the water and they baptize him inside it in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit (Mt 28:19). Restoration of the Holy Spirit in man. Christ sends an angel to guard the baptized.

baptism and made it so that it would dwell inside them to save them and protect them from the impure spirits, their enemies, because they have been expelled from them. If he believes and is baptized and professes that Christ is the Son of God, the Holy Spirit does not separate from him. The heavenly angel sent upon him by Christ at the moment of his baptism protects him. The heavenly angel will be inside the faithful to protect them until the day in which God decides their death. Fate of the baptized faithful and the infidel.

39 If his (the faithful’s) blood diminishes and he sees the evil appearance of the impure spirits that fill the air (Eph 6:12), he will fear them and his blood will dry up; his soul will separate from his body and that heavenly angel who was with it (the soul) from the day of his baptism will receive it and will lead it where it deserves. If, however, the baptized person renounces the Son of God, the Holy Spirit and the heavenly angel who protects him separate from him. That impure spirit that went out of him on the day of his baptism will not want to return to him alone but will go and take with him seven other impure spirits, worse than him, and they will enter and lodge in that apostate; his end will be worse than his beginning as the holy Gospel says (Mt 12:45 // Lk 11:26). The faithful must reject the author of the lying homily.

40 O beloved, it is necessary that (the believers) accept these testimonies of the prophets and of the holy Gospel and that they do not accept the word of that author of the lying homily. He who has a mind and the knowledge of the Holy Scriptures should not believe that it is the holy angels of God who kill humans. Indeed, death is terror, as David the prophet says: “For the terror of death has descended upon me” (Ps 55:4). As for the holy angels of God, in them there is no terror, and they do not frighten anyone; rather, they remove fear from the one to whom they appear.

### Testimony of Saint Anthony

41 As our father, clothed with the Holy Spirit, Abba Anthony was a witness and said that the holy angels do not terrorize any of those to whom they appear, and that rather they remove fear from them as did the angel who appeared


[p. 486] {#po.59.p486}
ገብረ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘአስተርአዮ' ፡ ለዘካርያስ ፡ ‹ወአኀዘ ፡ ይቤሎ" ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ኦዘካርያስ' ። ወከማሁ ፣ ይቤላ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፣ ኦማርያም ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ እዜንወክሙ ፣ ፍሥሐ ፡ ዐቢየ' ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤሎን ፡ ለአንስት ፡ ለእለ" ፡ መጽአ ፡፣ ኀበ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፣ኢትፍራሃ ፡፣ አንትን ። ወይቤ' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ኩሉ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ እመኖፍስተ ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ ናሁ ፡ አጠየቀ ፡ ወከሠተ ፣ ከመ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አልቦ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ፍርሀተ" ።
to Zechariah and began to say to him: “Do not fear, O Zechariah!” (Lk 1:13). He said the same to Mary: “Do not fear, O Mary” (Lk 1:30); he said the same to the shepherds: “Do not fear, for I announce to you a great joy” (Lk 2:10). He said the same to the women who came to the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ: “Do not fear!” (Mt 28:5). Saint Anthony said: “All terror comes from the impure spirits.” Thus he has clarified and expressed that there is no terror with the holy angels of God.

### Death comes from the impure spirits, the Son saved us

42 David the prophet said: “For death is terror” (Ps 55:4), and if there is no terror with the angels, then the angel of God does not cause death, because death is terror, but death and terror come from the impure spirits who are enemies. From them the Son of God saved us when he came and was crucified and died for us; he demanded from them the ransom with the cost of his death and took us at the price of his immolation.

43 The apostle Paul, in his letter to the Hebrews, manifests that the devil has the power of death and that Christ liberated them from him with his death, when he says about Christ that “with his death he destroyed the one who has the power of death in his hands, and this is the devil, and healed those who were under the terror of death for all their days” (Heb 2:14-15). If Paul so declares, that is, that the devil is the power of death and that our Lord destroyed him with his death and saved those who believed in him by virtue of his authority, and that death is terror, behold now that he has lied who says that death is a pure angel. 5 2 You judge that among the Christians of this era, disparate discourses on the correct faith have multiplied. Each of them has their own position that does not agree with the position of the other; rather, they deny it. But you withdraw from this and are anxious. Do not marvel at this, because the reason for their foolishness is in their language. Indeed, after the Arabic language defeated them and none of them remained who understood what was read to them in church 10 in the Coptic language, they used to listen without understanding. For this reason, the knowledge of the Christian faith was taken from them.

3 The bishops of Egypt were the teachers of all the bishops of all the ends of the entire earth, and the Egyptians were the heads of all the councils of the correct faith. Indeed, for the great synod of the 318 who gathered at Nicaea, their head was Alexander, the nineteenth of the patriarchs of Egypt in the city of Alexandria. And for the second synod of the 150 who gathered at Constantinople, their 5 head was Abba Timothy, patriarch of Egypt in the city of Alexandria. And for the third synod of the 200 bishops who gathered at Ephesus, their head was Abba Cyril, also patriarch of Egypt in the city of Alexandria. The fourth synod of the 630 of those who gathered at Chalcedon took place without truth, and they divided the Only-Begotten Son of God; they made him of two natures. Dioscorus, patriarch of Egypt and of the city of Alexandria, did not agree with them; rather, 10 he excommunicated them, manifested to them their heresy and their rebellion against the heavenly king for fear of the earthly king. Heroism of the Christians of Egypt.

4 Indeed, in all eras, the Egyptians profess the truth and detest rebellious kings and authorities; they profess the truth, in every era they have shown patience until the shedding of their blood and the plundering of their assets. Because of the greatness of their zeal for the Orthodox faith, when the Greek rulers 20 decided to change the Orthodox faith, (the Egyptians) delivered themselves to the Muslim Arab rulers. They were patient and submitted to them, they gave them taxes. They did all this so as not to agree with the Greeks in the corruption of the Orthodox faith.

confirm you and manifest to you the truth from the Holy Scriptures with the help of the Holy Spirit. You say that among them there are those who maintain that the Holy Trinity is one (single) Person and that Christ is the Father and the Son is the Holy Spirit. This word took hold among them because of their intense interaction with the Muslims, and they did not understand what was read to them 5 every day in church in the Coptic language, that God is the Holy Trinity, the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit.

6 The Father is the Father, and is not the Son, and the Son is the Son and is not the Father: also the Holy Spirit is the Holy Spirit and is not the Father, nor the Son, but is the Spirit of the Father and of the Son. He is their life because He is their Spirit, he dwells inside them without division or separation, he proceeds from them without division because this is the Holy Trinity that is, and that will be forever. The Father never existed, not even for the blink of an eye, without his Son; and the Father and the Son never existed, not even for the blink of an eye, without the Holy Spirit, but these three exist always, without alteration.

### Person of the Holy Spirit in his essence
ተወልደ ፡ ወኢተፈልጠ፤ ፡ ፣እምአካለ ፡ አብ! ፡ ወትረ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ካልእ ፡ እምአካለ' ፡ አብ' " ‹ወአካለ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፣ ካልእ ፡ እምአካለ ፣ አብ ። ወካልእ ፡ እምአካለ' ፡ ወልድ ፡ አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘየኀድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ "አካለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አካለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትድምርት ። ወይሠርፅ ፡ እም ኔሆሙ ፣ በከመ ፡ ፳ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለአብ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወውእቱ ፣ የሕይወቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወውእቱ'' ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወበተዋሕዶቱ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ።
The Son was born in his Person and has never separated from the Person of the Father, and He is different from the Person of the Father; also the Person of the Holy Spirit is different from the Person of the Father, and different from the Person of the Son, but it is he (the Holy Spirit) who dwells in the Person of the Father and in the Person of the Son, without union 5 and proceeds from them, as one of them, without division because he is the Spirit of the Father. He is also the Spirit of the Son, and He is the life of the Father, and he is also the life of the Son because of his unity inside them.

8 We have ascertained that They together with Him are: one (single) God, one (single) 10 power, one (single) energy, one single divinity, one single substance, and the Spirit is life, because the Spirit is the author of all that is living. If therefore one (single) is the life of the two Persons, they are one (single) life, one (single) operation, one (single) will, one (single) power. It is indeed the Spirit that fulfills all this, who is one who witnessed the trinity of the Persons. The holy Gospel tells of John the Baptist, that when he baptized Christ, Son of God, he saw the Holy Spirit while descending upon him from the heavens in the form of 15 a dove, and heard the voice of the Father saying: “This is my Son whom I love and in whom I am well pleased” (Mt 3:17). Behold, in this, the three Persons existing and who will exist forever have been manifested to us, and they do not change. Again on unity in distinction.

became human, and it is he who before becoming incarnate existed in his invisible Person. And the Holy Spirit in his Person in the likeness of a dove (Mt 3:16), without the Person of the Father, is invisible and not incarnable. He manifested himself, however, to John in this form to assure him that it is his peculiar Person, just as the Person of the Son is peculiar to Him alone. In the same 5 way, the voice of the Father was heard from heaven in his Person.

### Elucubrations on the relationships between the three divine Persons

Scriptural witnesses on intra-trinitarian relationships. 10 If the Father is not circumscribable by the word, since He has neither body nor word, except the Son who is his Word, He manifested himself to John with this discourse to confirm to him that he possesses his (own) Person, without those two Persons that he saw, even if they are from Him, without 10 separation. Indeed, the Holy Spirit proceeded from the Father to the Son, just as John saw him descend from the Father from heaven upon the Son, since it is He who exists in this way and who will be from the Father to the Son, and is in the Father and in the Son. There is much of this in the holy Gospel that ensures certainty about the Father and the Son as the Son used to speak to the Father and the Father to answer him to confirm their Person, as Saint John says in the gospel, that the 15 Son said: “O Father, glorify your son” (Jn 12:28) and the Father from heaven said: “I have glorified and will glorify again” (Jn 12:28). This is not a novelty in the holy Gospel: the three Persons exist and will exist for eternity. Synergy between the Father and the Son in creation.

created the light”, that is to say, God the Son, whom God created, that is, whom God creates, and then God the Father saw what he had created, the Son God, that it was good. Thus he says in the Book of Creation regarding all that he created, that the Father “said” and the Son realized, and the Father “saw” and “made good”. On the creation of man, he manifested this and made it clear when God 5 said: “Let us make man according to our image, our likeness, and our face”. This means that the Father spoke in this way to the Son who existed and will exist with him.

### God created man in the image of God but Adam transgresses

10 12 He says again: “God created man in the image of God” (Gen 1:27); this means that God created him in the image of the Son through whom he manifested himself. He says again: “When Adam ate from the tree and thought that he would become God, he became naked and his image changed” (Gen 3:10). When God saw him in that shameful image, he said: “Behold, Adam has become 15 like one of us” (Gen 3:22). He pronounced this word to the Son and to the Holy Spirit who are God like Him, mocking Adam because he “wanted to become God like one of us and (instead) has reduced himself thus” (Gen 3:22).

In Psalm 10 109 he says: “The Lord said to my Lord: sit at my right hand until I have placed your enemies under the footstool of your feet” (Ps 109:1).

As for what you said 5 regarding the Melkites, their faith is evil, different from the Jacobites of the correct faith.

17 I explain to you therefore the origin of the transgression and corruption of the Melkites, that is, that the trunk of the Christian faith is that the only-begotten Son, born of the Father before the world was created, is One of the Holy Trinity. Light 188 light, God from God truly, one (single) Person extended and subtle who is illimitable and incontenible, the existing one, one among the three Persons. He became incarnate in human flesh from the Holy Spirit and from the Virgin Mary, not (becoming) like animal flesh, because animal flesh is only flesh, bones, and blood without a rational and speaking soul. The perfect human body is indeed flesh, bones, and blood, and a rational and speaking soul. Equally, our Lord Jesus 1Eristo took a perfect human flesh like this; he took it from the Virgin Mary without human seed.

18 He did not make (his body) descend from heaven together with him, as the followers of the creed of Udasis maintain, which the second synod gathered at Ephesus excommunicated. They wrote to us that when they maintain this, (they mean to say) that the Word of God, the Son of God, became one with this human flesh, one perfect one. This is the faith of the disciples, the holy apostles, and of those who came 5 after them, centuries after centuries until the days of Theodosius “the younger”, king of Constantinople.

19 There was a patriarch in Constantinople, one named Nestorius, of a corrupt heart, without a spiritual mind, who exceeded in investigating the incarnation of the Son of God. And when he knew that the Son of God is a perfect, spiritual, extended Person, existing before the world was created, and that he took from Mary the perfect humanity and that each humanity had an (autonomous) existence, Nestorius did not possess a mind that understood how they became (one), the Person of before with the new Person.

20 That renegade used to preach and in his instructions taught that Christ was two persons and two natures. When the doctrine of this person became manifest, the synod of the 200 bishops gathered against him and their head was Cyril, patriarch of Alexandria. When they heard his evil creed that professed that Christ was two essences and two natures, they excommunicated 5 and removed him. He had indeed declared that Mary was not the Mother of God, because Mary did not beget God at the beginning, because at the beginning he was born from his Father without a mother, before the world was created. When Mary became the mother of God, since God was one with his humanity from which he was generated by way of the unification of God with his humanity from which he was generated, we have the certainty that She became truly the mother of God. 10 If Nestorius maintains that Christ was two essences and two natures, how then did the divinity become one with his humanity from which he was born, as that excommunicated one (Nestorius) maintains, who is not the mother of God. Marcian and the Council of Chalcedon.

of the word of Nestorius and to confirm the (other) half. In fact, they acknowledged that Christ is one (single) Person, that is to say truly (one), while they resembled Nestorius in the two natures. They added of their own accord what Nestorius had not professed, namely two wills, two operations. And they maintained this, that Christ is one Person and two natures, two wills, two operations. This is 5 the faith of Nestorius, but they disguised it with one Person and with Mary, in their statement that Mary is the Mother of God in the Person and in the natures, or in the Person, without the natures.

### Arguments against the Christology of Nestorius
"ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በህላዌ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጠባይዓት'' ፡ ይደልዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበሉ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ማርያም ፡ መንፈቁ ፡ አምሳክ ፡ ወመንፈቁ' ፣ ፍጡር ። እስመ ፡ ህላዌሁ'' ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወጠባይዓቱ ፡ ተፈጥረት" ። ወለእመ ፣ ሃ ር ኮነ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ኢይደልዎሙ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ለወልደ"' ፡ ማርያም ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይስግዱ ፡ ለፍጡር ፡ ከመ'!' ፡ ፈጣሪ ። ምንት" ፡ ዘአዖረ ፡ አልባቢ ሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ፳ ፡ በ፪ ፡ ጠባይዓት ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ተውሕዱ" ፡ ፪ ፡ ህላዌ”! ፡ ወኢተውሕደ'' ፡ ፲፪ ፡ ጠባይዓት" ። ወእፎ ፡ ይኩን!" ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፪ ፡ ፈቃድ ፡ ወንሕነሰ” ፡ ነአ ምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መንፈሳዊያን ፣ ተለዐሉ?" ፡ እምጠባይዓተ ፡ ሥጋ ፣፡ ወአንሶሰዉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አብሕርት ፡ ወሠረሩ ፡ በነፋስ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ” ፡ ንስቲተ ፣ እምጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይገብር" ፡ ዘንተ” ፡ ብእለ ፡ ተወ ልዱ” ፣ እምዘርአ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ንብል” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትስብ እት፤' ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ እምዘርአ ፡ ሰብእ”' ፡ ወተው ሕደ” ፣ አምላክ ፡ ቃል ፡ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ህላዌ ።
22 If it were in the Person without natures, they would have to profess that the Son of Mary is half God and half creature, in that in his Person he is God, while his natures were created. If the Son were thus, they should not worship the Son of Mary, lest they worship the creature on the same level as the Creator. What has ever blinded their hearts when they professed that the Person of Christ is one (single) in two natures? And how could the two Persons have been united without the two natures having been united? And how could there be two wills in the Son of God, while we know that many 15 spiritual saints rose above the nature of the body and moved above the seas and hovered over the air? If a little of the grace of the Holy Spirit accomplishes this in those who are born from the seed of man, equally, what shall we say about the humanity of him who was born of the Holy Spirit and not from the seed of man and to whom the only Word of God was united (Lk 1:35; Jn 1:14), from the perfect essence? 20

### Against Nestorius with arguments drawn from the natural world
ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ትትቴየል፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ይትቃረና ፡ አላ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብላዕ ፡ ወይስተይ ፡ አው ፡ ይስክብ ፡ አው ፡ ይትራከብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትከልኦ ፡ ሃ እምዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ቃል ፡ ጥኑ ፣ ኢይክል"፤ ፣ የአምላክ ፣ ቃል! ፣ ከመ ፣ ይረሲ ፡፣ ጠባይዓተ ፡ ትስብእት ፡ ትኩን ፡ አሐደ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ተዋሕዶተ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ንሕነ' ፡ ንሬኢ ፡ ላህበ ፡ እሳት ፡ በጠባይዓት፤ ፣ ሶበ ፣ ትሳተፍ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቁሪር ፡ በጠባይዓቱ' ፡ ትሬሲዮ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ፣ጠባይዓት ፡ ወፅ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ለእሳት ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ ምንተ ፡፣ ንብል ፡ በእንተ ፣ አምላክ ፡ ቃል ፣ ይትመዋእኑ ፡ እምትስብእቱ"' ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በህላዌ ፡ በከመ ፣ ይትዋሐድ ፡ በጠባይዓት ፡ ወፈቃድ ፡ ወግብር ። ወለእመ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ከመ" ፣ ንቤ'! ፡ ፪ ፡ ጠባይዓት ፣ ኮነ'' ፡ ፳ ፡ እምደመርነ'' ፡ መለ ኮተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ትስብእቱ'' ፡ ወንቶስሖሙ ። ሐሰው ፡ በዝንቱ ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንሬኢ ፡ ጠባይዓተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጠባይዓተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይደመሩ ፡ ወኢይቶስሑ" ። እስመ ፡፣ ለእመ ፡ ተደመሩ ፡ ወተቶስሑ ፡ እማሰኑ" ፡ ክልኤ ሃ1[7ገሐ ሆሙ ፣ እስመ ፡ ማይ ፡ ያጠፍእ ፡ እሳተ ፣ ወይከውን ፡ ምስሌሁ'' ፡ ሐመደ ።
23 And if the rational soul prevails over its flesh, its will cannot oppose it, but it desires to eat and drink, or to sleep, or to unite with its wife, despite the fact that (the soul) forbids him all these things. How much more can the humanization in which the Word of God subsists not render the nature of humanity one, together with the unity of his Person 5 in it? In fact, we observe that when the flame of fire in the natures interacts with water, cold in its nature, it makes it one with itself, a single nature and a single operation. And if this is the action of fire which has been created, what shall we say about the Word God? Will he perhaps be defeated by his humanization with which he became one in the Person, just as he unites in the natures, in the will, and in the action? If then they say that we declare that 10 18 two natures have become one (single), we would sum the divinity with his humanity and mix them. In this they lied, because we observe the natures of water together with the natures of fire without them summing, nor mixing. In fact, if they were to sum or mix, they would both destroy each other, because water extinguishes fire and together with it becomes ash. Again on the hiding of the Son from Satan 15

24 If they cling to his eating and his drinking, to his sleep, to his fatigue, to his fasting, to his weeping, to his passion, we know that he did not perform these actions because he had need of them. Far be it from him! He in fact performed these works to make himself like us and to hide himself 20 from Satan, so that (Satan) would not recognize him in his identity as God, and would not distance himself from him and would not dare to kill him and would not render his coming vain: for if he had not died, we would not be saved.

### The humanization of the Son is a solidarity with all humans

25 25 His coming would have been useless because he came to save us, and we do not deny that he is human flesh (which) assumes everything that man assumes in eating, in drinking, and in sufferings. We oppose those who maintain that he performed these actions by compulsion. If he did not eat, except because he was hungry, we observe that many saints in whom a little grace dwells, endure not eating and not drinking and overcome hunger and thirst 30 for many days.

26 It is necessary that He performed this by reason of his humanity which he had made one with his divinity, because He has power over everything. He did not eat and did not drink because he was hungry or thirsty, but he ate and drank to participate together with us in flesh and blood, so that we too might participate with him in the Holy Spirit (Heb 2:14-15). He ate bread 5 and we saw the body of his humanity, (exactly) as we see that our body (is founded) on the bread that we eat. Furthermore, He ate bread so that we could always find his body, with us through the bread. And likewise we, the water and the wine that we drink, it becomes blood for us. Equally, He used to drink water and blood so that from them he might draw his blood from them, because by obtaining them, we too could have his 10 blood with us. The union between divinity and humanity is realized in an identical manner in the Eucharist

5 29 How blind was the mind of those who maintained that he drank compelled by thirst. He with whom there is the water of life that quenches the thirst of whoever drinks it! This (same) Gospel also says that He did not eat because he was hungry. In fact, his disciples came to him and asked him to eat and our Lord Jesus Christ said to them: “As for me, I have food that you do not know” (Jn 4:31-32) and among themselves they said: “Has someone 10 brought him food to eat?” (Jn 4:31-33). He said to them: “My food is to do the will of him who sent me, and to fulfill his work” (Jn 4:31-34). He did not say: “My food is bread or other material foods”, because He did not need this.

15 30 If it had been because of hunger and thirst that he ate and drank, so we would also say, that He died and that like us his blood dried up, as the renegades maintain he was defeated by death and that his word was rescinded: “I lay down my life of my own (spontaneous) will, and there is no one who takes it from me, and I have the power to take it back” (Jn 10:17-18). If his word is not rescinded, it is not because of the weakness 20 of the natures that he died, just as he declared about himself. For this reason, he ate and drank without having had either hunger or thirst, (but only) by his will and by his power and not by compulsion, because nature does not defeat its Creator.

### The salvific death of Jesus was voluntary

31 His blood did not dry up before his death as (happens 25 with) us, but they pierced his side; an hour and a half after his death " flowed from him water and blood (Jn 19:34). We have demonstrated that He did not die by compulsion because of the drying up of the blood, but by his will and by his power; he bowed his head and separated the soul of his humanity (Jn 19:30) from his flesh voluntarily, without his blood drying up.

### Divinity and humanity in the person of Jesus Christ

32 In fact, his divinity is one with the soul of his humanity which descended into Sheol (1 Pt 3:18-19) and is one with his flesh which was crucified and buried. He is God in the flesh, (therefore) in the cross and in the tomb, he was without a rational, speaking soul. And He is God in the rational, speaking soul, in Sheol and in Paradise. He is God who became incarnate in the wood of the 5 cross, in the tomb, and is God in the humanization, in Sheol and in Paradise, so much does it confirm the unity of his humanity with his divinity, and this does not separate from him in every action, because it is the body of Christ. And (even) if it was material, circumscribed, since the soul became flesh, united with the divine word, extended together with it, supremely elevated, supremely profound. And if that flesh were limitable in a place, so would 10 it be of the soul of his humanity. It was with the Word God who fills every place and his unity that becomes thus, how can it be two natures, or two wills, or two operations?

If the evangelist John had said this about the faithful, 20 who in truth are born of the pleasure of man and of the blood of women, and in whom the will of the flesh in them is clear, (it would not be pertinent because) for the little grace in baptism that has descended upon them, they have separated themselves from every nature of theirs, from blood and from the will of the flesh and from the will of man.

36 The 318 declared the same in the Creed that “the Son is equal to the Father in his divinity through whom everything was realized, for us men and for our salvation he descended from heaven”. What is its interpretation 10 when they professed: “he descended from heaven (he who) is equal to the Father in his divinity?”. Was the earth perhaps empty of Him? Or is there perhaps a place from which He is absent? Or, is there perhaps a place that limits him? Can he be circumscribed or contained? They say this, just as the Gospel says: “the Word became flesh” (Jn 1:14) because he became one with the flesh, lived on earth in the womb of the Virgin, existing, present, visible, palpable, 15 therefore they say: “he descended”. In fact, he who exists invisibly, became this visible flesh, and this God who exists became man.

the Evangelist declare that “the Word became flesh” (Jn 1:14) and did not say “he became only in the Person”? He instead said: “He truly became flesh in everything that pertains to man”. He declared: “The Word became flesh and dwelt among us and we have contemplated his glory, as the glory of an only-begotten Son to his Father, full of 5 grace and of truth” (Jn 1:14). He demonstrated that He is the only-begotten Son in his divinity and in his humanity, unique to his Father, full of grace and of truth. The Son does not have two Persons, nor two natures. As he confirmed in the holy Gospel, the unity is (between) his humanity and his divinity when he maintains that no one has ascended to heaven except he who descended from heaven (Jn 3:13). 10

38 Question the Melkites on the interpretation of this word which says: “no one ascended except he who descended” (Jn 3:13). And if the flesh did not ascend, why then did the Gospel become a witness, when he said to them: “Touch me so that you may see that the spirit does not have flesh, nor bones, just as you see that I possess” (Lk 24:39). And they touched him and he ascended to heaven 15 before their eyes (Acts 1:9). He has therefore clarified that the flesh ascended to heaven. He also said: “No one ascended to heaven except that which descended from heaven” (Jn 3:13). What do they answer in this regard? “Does the word of our Lord among them perhaps lie?”. Far be it from Him! If we say that the flesh descended from heaven, what induced him to desire Mary? “And behold he ascended to heaven”. And he declared: “No one ascended to heaven except that which descended from 20 heaven” (Jn 3:13). What is the interpretation of the expression that “He descended from heaven, and that He does not descend from her (Mary)” but that he became one with his divinity which is in heaven, the unity of essence and of nature. To ascertain his unity he made it so that He descended from heaven and that He did not descend from her (Mary).

40 We however affirm and profess that that flesh from the Virgin Mary has truly become one nature and one Person with Him who descended from heaven. As he said about the Word, citing the evangelist, that the Word became 5 flesh, and did not say “the flesh descended from heaven”. Where are the two natures? And in what way do they separate them? Behold then that the evangelist has confirmed to us that God became flesh without change. As for man, he became God without change, the Creator became like a creature and the creature like the creator. The celestial became terrestrial and the terrestrial became celestial. The old became new and the new became old. The Son of God became Son of Mary, and the 10 Son of Mary became Son of God who was born of God before the world was created. He was born of Mary with a carnal birth, and the flesh that was born of Mary, was that which was born of God. As God said of Him by the tongue of David: “My son you are, and I today have begotten you” (Ps 2:7).

41 Thus is dismissed the doctrine of those who believed in two natures and in two Persons of God who became man. How great is the statement of our father Abba Cyril on the unity of the soul with the body and this is 20 an analogy of the unity of God with his humanization. In fact, the soul together with the body is one nature, obtained from two natures. No action of the soul is known apart from the body, nor action of the body except through the soul. Unique is their nature obtained from the two natures.

5 43 You instead maintain that the soul and the body are two natures. Then you should declare that Christ is of three natures. One nature, that which existed; then two natures, the soul and the body. You maintain that the soul and the body are two natures: here you should declare that the soul and the body are one nature from two natures so that they do not have to declare that in Christ there are three natures. If they confess that the soul and the body are one nature obtained from the two 10 natures, they must declare that they are God and humanity. If the single nature is separated then the humanity performed divine actions and God performed actions of the flesh. Pronouncements on water and fire, since in water is found fire, and if fire is found, water is found. How great is the word of the apostle Paul: “Since the first Adam was a living soul while the second was a life-giving spirit, the first of the soul, the second spiritual” (1 Cor 15:45). as the corpses that when they rise from the dead become perfect in their four natural elements, and yet their natures and their operations are spiritual. They will not have hunger, nor will they suffer thirst (Rev 7:16), they do not eat, they do not drink, they do not sleep, they do not tire, they do not undergo change, they do not become old, they do not wear out and they do not destroy themselves, because their soul will become 5 spiritual and will prevail over the flesh and the flesh will live for the life of the soul. Their life and the life of their soul will consist in their seeing God (Mt 5:8), just as in this world the flesh prevails over the soul, and his soul lived in his life and he does not live through his life.

10 45 In the same way, on the day of the resurrection the flesh will live for the life of the soul. As Paul says on the resurrection, if the created soul will prevail over the flesh it will make it rich from its nature and from its work (1 Cor 15:42-44). All the more God creator who united himself to this flesh in his essence, will this flesh not perhaps be superior and stronger than the soul that was created and will remove from the flesh the weak nature and together 15 with it will render it one (single) nature, one (single) will, one (single) action? This explanation alone is sufficient to invalidate the reasoning of those who believe in two natures and in two wills. It empties their pretext that uses eating, drinking, sleeping, fatigue and the other works of his that he performed to hide his divinity from Satan. But if he thought that the divinity did not unite with his humanity until he rose from the dead, no one believes this word. 20

the divinity that was united with him, without the weakness of the nature with which he became a single Person. They make the soul of the flesh that is from pleasure and sin stronger than the divinity, because the soul of the flesh that is from pleasure, when it rises from the dead, will have the power to remove from its flesh the weakness of the nature. And if it were as they maintain, that the nature remained in its weakness, it is because they have computed it with the divine nature, a single nature.

### Summary of the Christological debate and exhortation

47 I have therefore manifested to you, O beloved, the corruption of those who believe in the evil faith. I have manifested to you the truth by drawing from the Holy Scriptures and from the spiritual mind, an exposition that does not require further (explanations) beyond it. Understand it and observe it and be firm in it, verify what I have manifested to you about the pride of the Orthodox Christian faith (which is) above all faiths, because the faith of the Jacobites is more glorious, more precious and superior to all Christian faiths, because the faith of the Jacobites is truly the right faith that the holy Gospel and the holy Apostles preached. The “Jacobite” Egypt together with Syria, Armenia and the Ethiopians, Jacobites before the Syrians and Armenians 48 It is this holy and right faith that Satan wanted to destroy, until Dioscorus, patriarch of Egypt, defeated him and dispersed his council. Know that the Egyptians are always of the right faith, firm in the true faith, and in this faith Syria and Armenia agreed with them, and the Ethiopians are Jacobites together with us from the beginning, before those. Therefore God by the tongue of the prophet Isaiah (Is 19:24) says that Israel will be the third of (behind) the Egyptians and the Syrians. And he says thus: “Blessed be my people who are in Egypt”, that is the Egyptians, “and my inheritance Israel” (Is 19:25). Every blessing be upon the Egyptians and the Syrians who lived in the right faith, and upon the children of Israel who

agreed with them among all the peoples who contemplate God in the light of the mind and believed in him. Indeed, the interpretation of Israel is ‘he who sees God’. *And thus he says about the synod of Chalcedon: “The ships of Chalcedon disappeared” (Is 23:1, 14) because of the wickedness of its knights and their councils, that is, the synod in which they gathered*. 5. Doxology
‹ወኩሉ፤'! ፡ አኩቴት ፡ ወስብሐት ፣ ወክብር ፡ ወዕበይ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፣ ንፈኑ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ዘኀረየነ ፣ ሃ 50፡: ለሃይማኖቱ"! ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወለአቡሁ ፡ መሐሪ ፡ ወለመንፈስ'' ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ምኅ የዊ ፡ ዘዕሩይ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በመለኮት ፡ ወይእዜኒ'"' ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለም ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
49 It is our duty to raise all thanksgiving, praise, honor and greatness to our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of God who chose us for his right faith, and to his merciful Father and to the life-giving Holy Spirit who is equal to him in divinity, now and forever and unto the ages of ages, amen.

### HOMILY XI
ድርሳን ፡ዓሠርቱ ፡ወአሐዱ ።
Proem and anamnesis of the previous instructions
ድርሳን ፡ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ወአሐዱ፤' ። በእንተ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወማርያም ፣ እኅቴ" ፡ ወመዝሙር" ፡ ዘ ፻ቋ፳፤ ፡ ወዘ፻2፳' ፣ ወራእይ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ ዘዳንኤል ፡ ወስብሐተ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ደቂቅ ። ኦእጐ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያብርህ ፣ አዕይንተ ፡ ልበከ' ፡፣ በብርሃነ ፡ መንፈስ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ትለቡ ፣ ምሥጢራተ" ፣ መለኮቱ ። ትቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ አንበብከ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ዘጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሠናየ ፣ በኀቤከ ፡ ወተፈወስከ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሰአልከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጽሐፍ'' ፣ ለከ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወማርያም" ፣ እኅቱ ። ወናሁ ፡ ተሠጠውኩከ ፡ ለዘሰአልከኒ'' ፡ ወጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ" ፡ “01፡9. ትርጓሜ!" ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወማርያም" ፣ እኅቱ ፡ ወወሰክኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ሃ ትርጓሜ ፣ ፪'"መዝሙር ፡ ቫዝ፻ወወሮ ፡ ወዘ፻ወቋ” ፡ ወራእይ” ፡ ራብዕ ፣
1 Eleventh treatise. On the interpretation of the Canticle of Moses and of Mary his sister, of Psalm 135 and of (Psalm) 150, of the fourth Vision of Daniel, and of the praise of the three youths. O beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your mind with the light of the Holy Spirit the Comforter so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity. You say you have read the interpretation of the book of the Torah and of Joshua, son of Nun, which I wrote for you, and that it was good for you; that through it you were healed and you asked me to write for you the interpretation of the praise of Moses and of his sister Mary. Now I have answered what you asked me and I have written for you in this book, the interpretation of the praise of Moses and of his sister Mary and I have added for you the interpretation of two Psalms, 135 and 150, and of the “fourth vision of Daniel”, as well as of the “praise of the three youths”. Recall of the use of typological exegesis
ዘዳንኤል ፡፣ ወስብሐተ ፡ ፻ ደቂቅ ። ወእወጥን” ፡ ለከ ፡ በትርጓሜ ፣ የስብሐተ ፣ ሙሴ”! ። ቀዳሚ ፡ ከሠ ትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አገበርዎሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወያሰጥሙ” ፡ ውሉ ዶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወለሠራዊቱ" ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያጌብርዎሙ-”፤ ፣ ለደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ ውስተዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወያሰጥምዎሙ-” ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወኮነ ፡ አድኅኖቶሙ” ፡ በእደ ፡ ሙሴ" ፡ በከመ ፡ አድ ኅኖተኝ ፣ የደቂቀ ፡ አዳም” ፡ በእዴሁ'' ፣ ለእግዚእነ'፤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክር 16፡95 ስቶስ” ። ወስጥመቱ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በከመ ፡ ስጥመቱ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ በስቅለተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
2 I begin therefore for you, the interpretation of the praise of Moses. At the beginning I explained to you in the book that Pharaoh and his army who oppressed the children of Israel in Egypt and drowned their children in the sea (Ex 1:22) were the type of the devil and his army who oppressed the children of Adam in this world and drowned them in Sheol. Their salvation happened by the hand of Moses, as the salvation of the children of Adam (happened) by the hand of our Lord Jesus Christ and the sinking of Pharaoh and his army in the sea is like the sinking of the devil and his army in Sheol by means of the “crucifixion of Christ.

### Exhortation to the disciple
ለቡ ፡፣ ትርጓሜ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘሰብሐ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ሙሴ፤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሃ 15ነ/ጩከ ተሰጥሙ ፣ ፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሠተ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰባሕ ፡ ቦቱ' ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ተሰጥመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፣፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለዘርኡ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ጽሑፍ ፡ በፀአት ፡ እምኦሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዐርጉ ፡ እም ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተሰጥመ ፣፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ሰብሐ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ስብሐት' ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበሉ ፡ ንሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ በስባሔ ፡ ተሰብሐ ። ፈረሰ ፡ ወመስተጽዕናነ ፡፣ ወረዎሙ!" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወዘሰ ፣ ዘይቤ ፣ እስመ ፣ በስብሐት" ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ ከሠተ ፣ በዝንቱ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ በዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀሉ' ፡ በአጥፍኦተ" ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡፣ በአድኅኖተ" ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ አዳም ፡ እምግብርናቲሆሙ'' ። እስመ ፡ ከማሁ'! ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ይሰሚ ፡ ስቅለቶ ፡ ስብሐት"'' ፡ ሎቱ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወንገለ'' ፡ ሃ ዮሐንስ ፣ እስመ ፡ ዮኖኖዊያን ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ለበዓል ፡ ፈተው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይር አይዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ አይድፅዎ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በጽሐ ፣ 46ኮ ሰዓት ፡ ዘይሴባሕ ፡ ወልደ'" ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው!'' ፡ የአማን ፡ አማን" ፣ እብለክሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኅጠተ ፡ ስርናይ ፡፣ ለእመ ፡ ኢወድቀት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡
3 Pay attention to this praise that Moses raised after Pharaoh and his army sank into the sea. In it, in fact, he manifested what it is our duty to raise as praise to Christ, on his crucifixion, through which the devil and his army sank into Sheol and he saved from him, Adam and his lineage. It is written in the Exodus of the Torah that when the children of Israel emerged from the Red Sea, after Pharaoh and his army had sunk, then Moses and the children of Israel praised God with this praise. Christological reading of Exodus 15:1-4. Exegesis of the terms “glory, glorification” 4 He reports that they said: “Let us praise God because he has been praised with praise, horse and rider he has thrown into the sea” (Ex 15:4). In reference to what he said: “Because with praise he was praised”, in this he manifested the praise of God on the wood of his cross, with the destruction of the devil and his army, with the ransom of the children of Adam from their slavery. Indeed our Lord, to him be glory!, used to call his crucifixion “glory” (Jn 12:23, 32). As the Gospel of John says that when the Greeks came to the feast, they wanted to see him and when his disciples informed him of this, he said: “The hour has come for the Son of Man to be glorified! Truly, truly I say to you that if the grain of wheat does not fall to the ground and die, it will remain alone but if it dies it will produce much fruit” (Jn 12:20-24).
ወኢሞተት ፡ ትተርፍ ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ ወእመሰ ፡ ሞተት'' ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ትፈሪ ። ከሠተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፡ ወሞቱ" ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ስብሐት ፡ ሎቱ ፣ እስመ ፣ ቦቱ ፡ ድኅነ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ፡ እሞት ፡ ወሲኦል ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ወምሕረት ፣ ወፍቅረ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ የዐምዖ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ። ወበውእቱ" ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ በስባሔ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘጸምአ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወይስተይ ። ይቤ ፡ ወንጌላዋ ፡ እስመ” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በእንተ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘይነሥእዎ ፡ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፤ ኢኮኑ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይሰባሕ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፣ እምቅድመ ፣ ይሰቀል ።
5 He manifested that his crucifixion and his death are for him a glory because through them Adam and his lineage were saved from death and from Sheol in justice, mercy and love of man without him committing injustice against the devil and his army and for this reason he was glorified with praise. Therefore when our Lord Jesus Christ says: “If anyone thirsts, let him come to me and drink” (Jn 7:37), the evangelist maintains that He was declaring this in reference to the Holy Spirit that those who believe in him would receive. Indeed they had not received it, because this (was pronounced) before he was glorified, that is, before he was crucified (Jn 7:39).

### Fulfillment of the prophecies

6 And when he entered Jerusalem riding a donkey, the holy Gospel pronounces this word, that the prediction of the prophet Zechariah was being fulfilled which says: “Do not fear, O daughter of Zion, behold your king comes to you riding on a donkey's colt” (Mt 21:5 // Zec 9:9). The holy Gospel says it, and at the beginning they did not understand that they prophesied this prophecy concerning him, but when he was glorified, that is, when he was crucified, then they understood it. Furthermore, in the Gospel he declares that our Lord Jesus Christ said: “O my Father, glorify your Son! And from heaven came a voice that said ‘I have glorified it and will glorify it again’” (Jn 12:28). Our Lord explained the meaning of the word of his Father “and I will glorify it again”. And our Lord said: “The moment of judgment of this world has come! Now the prince of this world is being cast out. And I, when I am lifted up from the earth, will draw everyone to me” (Jn 12:31-32). He confirmed that his glory (would happen) when he would be lifted up from the earth on the wood. The assault of the devil on Jesus on the wood of the cross and the consequences 7 And the devil, the prince of this world (Jn 14:30), comes to him to kill him, as he used to kill others; but our Lord, with the power of his divinity, placed him in the abyss of Sheol, just as he placed Pharaoh and his army in the sea. He drew to himself all the children of Adam, the dead and the living, over whom he (the devil) had reigned, by the obedience to him of Adam, their father. In the same way, he called him “prince of this world” (Jn 12:31). This is the glory of our Lord with the destruction of the enemies in truth. It is more precious and superior to the glory for the destruction of Pharaoh and his army, because the devil and his army are stronger knights than Pharaoh and his army. Their destruction and their descent to the underworld is superior to the defeat of Pharaoh and his army and their drowning in the sea. In the Canticle of Moses he has therefore confirmed the expression “Let us praise God, who has been truly exalted; he has thrown horse and rider. For my salvation he has become for me help and hiding place. He is my God and I glorify him.


[p. 542] {#po.59.p542}
አምላከ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወአሌዕሎ ። አጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘተለዐለ ፡ ዲበ' ፡ መስ 0147ቄ. ቀል ፣ ወረዎ፤ ፣ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቴ' ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ውእቴ፤' ፡ አም ላኩ ፡ ወአምሳከ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወኖኅ ፡ ወአብርሃም ፡ ወይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕ ቆብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፡ ተስእሎ' ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ዘህልው ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘበአማን ፣ ዘይሄሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይሴባሕ" ፡ ወየዐቢ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ እሴብሖ" ፡ ወአምላከ'' ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወአሌዕሎ ።
Exegesis of: “He is the God of my father” (Ex 15:2) 8 “He is the God of my father, and I exalt him” (Ex 15:1-2). He ascertained that he who was raised on the cross and threw the devil and his army into Sheol is his God and the God of his father Adam, Noah, Abraham, Isaac and Jacob. He is he who exists eternally as he asked him about his name and he said to him: “I am he who exists eternally” (Ex 3:14), since He is he who truly exists together with his Father God who is glorified and magnified, as Moses said: “He is my God and I praise him, and the God of my father and I exalt him.”

### Exegesis of Ex 15:3, 15:4-5, 15:6
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይቀጠቅጥ ፡ ጸብአ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ስሙ ። ለምንት ፡ ኦሙሴ ፡ ትሴብሖ ፡ ወለምንት ፡፣ ታሌዕሎ ። ወይቤ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ሰረገላቲሁ ፡ ሃ ,ዚ-› ለፈርዖን ፣ ወለኩሎ'' ፡ ሠራዊቱ'' ፡ ወረዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ፡ ኅሩያነ! ፤ ወመስተጽዕናነ' ፡ በመሥልስት ። ወተሰጥሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባባሕረ ፣ ኤርትራ"! ፡ ወደፈኖሙ ፡ ማዕበል ፡ ከመ ፡ እብን ። አጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፣ ይደልዎ!' ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወልዕልና ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ወረዎሙ ፡ ለጸላእያን ፣ ኀያላን ፣ ወመስተጽዕኖናን” ፣ ውስተ ፣ ማዕምቀ ፡ ሲኦል ፡፣ ወአውረዶሙ ፡፣ መት ሕተ ፣ ታሕቲት ፡ በመስቀሉ ፡ ወበክዕወተ ፡ ደሙ ፡፣ በፍትሕ ፡ ወበምሕረት ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ምስለ ፣ አብ” ፡ የማንከ ፡ እግ ዚኦ"! ፣ ተሰብሐ ፡ በኀይል ፡ የማነ ፡ እዴከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ሠረወቶሙ”" ፡ ለፀር ። ዝብሂል ፡ ጥማንከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የማኑ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወእዴሁ ፡ ወመዝራዕቱ ፡ ልዕልት” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተሰብሐ ፡ በኀ
9 “God crushes the battle, and his name is God” (Ex 15:2-3). Why, O Moses, do you glorify him? Why do you exalt him? Moses said: “The chariots of Pharaoh and all his army, the chosen third men he threw into the Red Sea, they were drowned in the sea, and the wave covered them like a stone” (Ex 15:4-5). He confirmed that to Him belongs glory and exaltation, because He threw the powerful enemies and the knights into the depth of Sheol and made them descend to the bottom of the abyss with his cross and with the shedding of his blood in justice and in mercy. Furthermore, Moses, conversing with the Father, said: “O Lord, your right hand has been glorified with power; your right hand has extirpated the enemy” (Ex 15:6). This means: “Your right hand is the Only-begotten Son”, since the Son is the right hand of the Father, he is his hand and his raised arm that was glorified in power when he destroyed the enemies of his Father with his cross, that is,

ይል ፣ ሶበ ፡ አጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለጸላእተ' ፡ አቡሁ ፣ በመስቀሉ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ፈርዖን ። እስመ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ኅሱር ፡ ድኩም" ፡ ሃ :)ሀከ ከመ ፡ ይኩን' ፡ ጸላኢሁ' ፡ ለአብ ፡ አላ ፡ ጸላኢሁሰ ፡ ዲያብሎስ' ፡ ወሠራ ዊቱ ፡ ዝአሥገሩ ፡ ፈጣሪሆሙ ፡ ለዘፈጠሮሙ ፡ በአርአያሁ ፡ ወበአምሳሊሁ' ፣ ወተመክነዩ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፣ ነግሥ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ በፍትሕ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈድዮሙ'" ፡ በፍትሕ ።
the devil and his army and not Pharaoh, because Pharaoh is despicable and weak to be an enemy of the Father, but his enemy is the devil and his armies who ensnared those whom their Creator created in his image and likeness (Gen 2:26-27) and led them into deception so much that they reigned over them in justice so that he would redeem them with justice. 5. Redemption of humans in the justice of God

10 And when they reigned over them with justice he would not have taken them from them with violence, and they, blind of mind, did not know that his disposition and his mercy are great. His wisdom is superior to their wisdom. He sends his Son so that he would become incarnate and act with them as they acted and take them away from them with justice to show his justice and his mercy and be glorified with power for the destruction of the enemies of his Father as Moses said.

He said again: “And with the spirit of your wrath, the water rose and the water compacted like a wall, and the storm settled in the middle of the sea. This word that says that the water rose and the storm settled has manifested to us that the calmed storm and the division of the sea became the destruction of Pharaoh and his army. Indeed, when Pharaoh saw that the sea was divided and that the children of Israel had passed through it, he dared to cross it to capture them and to destroy them but the calming of the storm and the division of the sea became his destruction.

12 Our Lord Jesus Christ did the same against the devil and his army, and when they approached him on the wood of the cross to kill him and to make his soul descend into Sheol, as they used to do against all the children of Adam, (Jesus Christ) encouraged them (to act) against him, when he said: “My God! My God! Why have you abandoned me?” (Mt 27:46). And again he said: “I thirst” (Jn 19:28). They dared against Him because of this word and thought that He was a man (born) from the pleasure of Adam their slave. They thought they could kill him. He dared against him as Pharaoh dared against Moses through the division of the sea. Equally, when the devil heard the word of our Lord saying: “My God! My God!” (Mt 27:46) he thought that He was a man (conceived) from the pleasure of Adam, and inside himself he said, as Moses said: “Pursuing I will take them and I will divide the booty, I will satisfy my soul, I will kill with my sword, and I will govern with my hand”. Hiding of Christ from the devil

13 Indeed he saw in our Lord actions that intimidated him and through them he knew that He was God and feared him. To the point that our Lord hid his divinity from him by means of weak human actions, for which he doubted him. And when he saw him perform wonders he feared and stood between doubt and belief. And he was not certain that he could never kill him, until he raised the Jews against him and against him they did what they did and he was not sure that the Jews had power over him. And when he saw him crucified, he approached him to test him, (to verify) if he was able to kill him.
