Indeed, I have never seen anyone among them one stronger than this. And when I have killed this one, my hand will reign and I will never return to be afraid again”. And immediately he approached him with temerity to intimidate him with his terrible and evil appearance, so that his blood would dry up and he would kill him.

### The devil succumbs to the power of the divinity of Christ
ወሶቤሃ' ፡ መጠወ" ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ነፍሰ" ፡ ትስብእቱ ፣ ፈቃዱ ፡ ዘእንበለ" ፡ ይየብስ'' ፡ 'ደሙ ፡ ወአኀዞ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ዋሕደ ፡ ምስለ'' ፡ ነፍሰ'' ፡ ትስብእቱ ፣ ወቀተሎ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ”! ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቅትለቱ ፣ ወአው ረዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ታሕቲት ። ምንት" ፡ ይሜኒ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ፈነውከ ፣ መንፈሰከ ፡ ወደፈኖሙ ፡ ባሕር ። ከሠተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ አውረዶ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ፍዳሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ህሂ ታሕቲት ፡ ወደፈኖ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ መትሕተ ፣ ታሕቲት ። ተደፍነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማዕምቀ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዐረር ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሰሮ ፡ ህየ'' ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ፍዳሁ ። ወማህረኮ”፤ ፣ ለአዳም ፡ ወለኩሉ” ፡ ዘርኡ ፣ ወአዕረጎሙ ፣ እምሲኦል” ፡ ታሕቲት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ በከመ ፡ አዕረጎሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምባ ሕር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ሲና ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ መኑ ፡ ይመስለከ ፡ እምነ ፡ አማልክት ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ከማከ ፡ ስቡሕ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ መንክር ፡ ስብሐቲከ ፡ ወትገ ብር”! ፡ መድምመ ። ዝብሂል ፡ መኑ ፡ እምነ ፡ አማልክት” ፡ አው ፡ አበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ እለ ፡ ተሰመዩ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወነቢያተ”' ፡ እለ ፡ ተሰመዩ”' ፡ አማልክተ” ። በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ረሰይኩከ ፡ አምላከ” ፡ ለፈርዖን ። ወበከመ ፣ ይቤ ፣ በመዝሙር ፡ በእንተ ፣ መሳፍንት" ፡ አንሰ ፡ እቤ ፡ አማልክት ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ይቤ” ፣ ሙሴ ፡ መኑ ፡ እምእሉ ፡ አማልክት ፣ ይመስለከ” ፣ እግዚኦ ፣ እስመ ፡ እሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ" ፣ ተሰመዩ ፡ አማልክተ ፡፣ በክብረ ፡፣ ዚአከ ፡ ሎሙ ።
15 And immediately, our Lord voluntarily surrendered the soul of his humanity, without his blood drying up and he captured with the strength of his divinity that was one with the soul of his humanity and killed the devil, instead of his killing and made him descend into the abyss of Sheol. How beautiful is the word of Moses that says: “You sent your spirit and the sea submerged them” (Ex 15:10). He manifested that when He surrendered his soul, he made the devil descend into the deep Sheol as his retribution and submerged him in the depths of the abyss. He was buried in the deep Sheol like lead, because God bound him there, as his retribution. Liberation of Adam from Sheol and ascent to Paradise 16 And he took as booty Adam and all his lineage and made them ascend from the deep Sheol to Paradise, just as he had made the children of Israel ascend from the sea to the desert of Sinai. For this Moses gave the perfection of the glorification saying: “Who among the gods resembles you O Lord, and who like you is glorified among the saints, your glory is wonderful and accomplishes the ineffable” (Ex 15:11). This means: “Who among the gods or among the ancient fathers who were called sons of God and prophets who were called sons of gods, as God said to Moses: ‘Behold I have made you God to Pharaoh’ (Ex 7:1), as he says in the Psalm concerning the princes: ‘But I say, You are gods’ (Ps 81:6 (LXX) // Jn 10:34)?” as Moses says: “Who among these gods resembles you, O Lord?” (Ex 15:11a). Indeed all these have been called gods, because you have honored them.

### Only God possesses the strength to save
ወአንተሰ' ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ በመለኮት፤ ፡ ወበባሕርይ ፡ ትገብር ፡ ኩሎ" ፡ በኅይልከ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሉንቱ ፡ "እስመ ፡ እሉንቱ' ፡ "በኅይልከ ፡ ኮኑ" ፣ ይገብሩ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘይመስለከ" ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኑ' ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ እሞት ፡ ወእምሲኦል ። ወኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ያድኅኑ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ እምዝንቱ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ባሕቲትከ ፡ አም ላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ወለእመ ፡ ተሠገውከ ፡ ወተመሰልከ ፡ በሰብእ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ 'እምኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን" ፡ ወአልቦ ፡፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘይሴባሕ ፡ ከማከ' ፡ ወኢይትነከር"' ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ በስብሐት ፡ ዘትገብር ፣
17 But you are truly God in divinity and in essence. You accomplish everything by your power, in truth, and not like these. Those indeed acted by your power and not by themselves and there is no one who resembles you, therefore they could not save the children of Adam from death and from Sheol and they did not succeed in saving themselves from that. But you are the only God in truth. And when you became incarnate you resembled man (Phil 2:7), to you belongs the glory “and there is among them no one who is glorified like You and none of them is admirable, because it is You who for glory accomplish wonders” (Ex 15:11b).

### Victory over the devil, foundation
of the salvation of humans
መንክራተ ። ዝብሂል ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወረዎ ፡ ሃ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ታሕቲት ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቅትለቱ'' ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ የማኖ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡፣ መስቀል ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ሰፋሕከ ፡ የማንከ'' ፡ ወወኅጠቶሙ ፡ ምድር ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአብ'' ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሠመርከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትቤዝዎ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በወ ልድከ ። ወሐደጎ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወውኅጠቶሙ ፣፡ ምድር ፣ ለጸላእትከ ፡ ህየንተ ፣ ቅትለቱ ፡ ወረሰየ'' ፣ ምድር'' ፡ ተኀጦሙ ፣ :0ቹ. በቦፍትሕ ። ወአዕረጎሙ ፡ ለአዳም ፡፣ ወለኩሉ" ፡ ዘርኡ ፡ ሙታነ'' ፣ እምሲ ኦል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ በምሕረቱ ።
18 What he called glory means that He was glorified when he threw the devil and his army into the deep Sheol in justice, instead of his killing, when he stretched his right hand on the wood of the cross. Therefore Moses said: “You stretched your right hand and the earth swallowed them” (Ex 15:12). He said this to the Father, since “when you agreed to redeem the world by means of your Son” (Jn 3:16), and let him stretch his hands on the cross The earth swallowed your enemies in exchange for his killing and made the earth swallow them with justice. By his mercy he made Adam and all his lineage ascend from Sheol to Paradise. Baptism “portal of salvation”

that you chose and fortified with your consolation in the rest of your temple” (Ex 15:13). This means that they are the people of the faithful, the living among the children of Adam that (Christ) guided by the hand of his disciples. And after his ascension to heaven he chose them for himself as his people and fortified them through the consolation of the Holy Spirit the Paraclete, and dwelt inside them for their faith. He made them for himself the hall of his temple (1 Cor 3:16-17) and rested inside them through their praises and sanctifications addressed to Him. Indeed, if they praise him continuously, the Holy Spirit is consolidated inside them and fortifies them against the army of the devil that envies them by reason of their ascent into their heavenly order. The army of Satan knew that the souls that ascended from Sheol could not access their heavenly order because their number did not equal that of the fallen demons; they do not ascend to it, until the children of Adam who die firm in the right faith had ascended there, and (gradually) their number was equal to theirs (of the fallen demons). Therefore the demons hated the faithful among the children of Adam and made sins pleasant to them and made them negligent in the Praise and in the Sanctification so that they would fall, as they fell and would not ascend to their order. Hermeneutics of Exodus 15:14-16a

21 Therefore Moses, after having said: “You guided your people” (Ex 15:13), says: “The peoples heard and were angered, pain seized the inhabitants of Philistia. On that day the princes of Edom trembled and the chiefs of Moab were seized by tremor, all those who lived in Canaan liquefied: fear and terror seized them” (Ex 15:14-16a). These are those whom he remembered, and they lived between the desert of Sinai and the land of Canaan. They prevented the children of Israel from passing until the land of Canaan (Dt 25:17; 1 Sam 15:2). And he says: “They were angered and agitated when they heard that the children of Israel were passing through their land”, and in the same way, the armies of Satan that are in the air, and those who dwell inside the idols, were frightened and terror seized them when they heard the preaching of his birth that destroyed them.

He redeemed the children of Adam from their slavery by means of holy baptism and through it he expelled them from them. 22 (Baptism) led them out of the houses of the idols inside which they worshipped them, and made them holy halls in which they celebrated to the Holy Triune. Moses also said: “All those who live in the land of Canaan melted, fear and tremor seized them” (Ex 15:15). What he said: “And all those who live in Canaan melted” (Ex 15:15), means, all the armies of Satan that fell from heaven, because the land of Canaan resembles the heavenly order from which the devil and his armies fell. As I explained to you in the (interpretation) of the book of the Torah, Canaan is the son of Ham, son of Noah and was blessed with his father. When he stared at his father and understood that the genitals of Noah his father were uncovered, he was cursed because he despised his father by looking at his father (Gen 9:20-27). At the beginning he was blessed and later he became cursed, like Satan who was blessed and fell from the blessing, when he despised his creator and did not praise him and did not sanctify him.

23 For this reason, the land of Canaan was likened to his order because he is the type of Canaan. Moses said: “Because the preaching of the apostles has melted all those who dwell in the land of Canaan”. This means: all the armies of the devil melted and weakened by virtue of holy baptism that melts their power and expelled them from the children of Adam and debilitated them with the Holy Spirit. Therefore Moses cursed them saying: “Fear and tremor seized them with the power of your arm, so that they would become stones, until your people that you, Lord, have acquired had passed” (Ex 15:16). He said: “With the power of your arm, abundant in power”, that is, with the Holy Spirit dwelling inside your people in baptism. He made fear and tremor descend upon them, upon all the armies of Satan and made them stones until your people had passed and had ascended into Paradise. Indeed the armies of Satan are the powers of the air (Eph 6:12) that worship the air and oppose the faithful in their life. When the soul of the faithful exits their bodies,

25 He also says: “Bring them in and plant them on the mountain of your sanctuary, and enter into your ready dwelling, which you, O Lord, have made. Your holy place, O Lord, which your hands have prepared. The Lord, you are king forever” (Ex 15:17-18). In this passage, the prophet Moses (Dt 18:18 // Acts 7:37) asks God to enable the faithful to defeat the armies of Satan and to help them commit themselves to his glorification until their death and ascent to Paradise, and that the number of the armies that fell from heaven might be completed and they might ascend to the heavenly order which is the mountain of the Lord's inheritance, and his ready dwelling which he made his holy abode prepared by his hands. Indeed, they did not ascend to the heavenly order until their number had been completed (balanced), in correspondence with the number of those who fell from heaven. For he redeemed them with his soul until they ascended to the heavenly order and gave the Holy Spirit to the living, those who were on earth, so that he might console them and strengthen them forever against the army of Satan. ከመ፤ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ በማእከለ ፣ ዓለም ፡ በኀይለ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያንሶስው ፡ ዲበ ፡ የብስ ፡ ወኢይሰጠሙ፤! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወጸላእቶሙ ፡ መኖፍስት ፡ ርኩሳን ፣ እለ ፡ ይሠርሩ ፡ ያሰጥሞሙ!፤ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነ 5‹ቄጀ‹: ወይሰብሮሙ ፡ እምላዕሌሆሙ ።
means that the faithful in the midst of the world moved on dry land by the power of the Holy Spirit and did not drown in Sheol, while their enemies, the unclean spirits that hover (in the air), the Holy Spirit drowns them and tears them away from them.

5 27 He says: “Mary, the prophetess, sister of Aaron, took a drum in her hands and behind her came all the women with drums and with many praises” (Ex 15:20). And Mary began before them saying: “Let us praise God because with praise he has been praised! He cast horse and rider into the sea” (Ex 15:21). In this passage, Mary refers to the community of the faithful upon whom the Holy Spirit descended (Acts 1:14; 2:1-4). For their true head 10 is the Virgin Mary, Mother of God. Indeed, it is she upon whom the Holy Spirit descended for the first time, as the angel Gabriel said to her (Lk 1:35). And she, the head of all believing women, just as Moses was the head of the men of the children of Israel and his sister Mary, the head of their women. This is the hermeneutics of the praise of Moses and his sister Mary. The interpretation of Psalm 135 15

28 And here is the interpretation of Psalm 135, which is as follows. Because he trusts in God and submits to him and praises him for what he had accomplished in the destruction of the Egyptians and in their sinking into the sea together with Pharaoh, their great king. (Psalm 135) is equal to the praise of Moses; however, he added the repetition: “For his mercy is eternal” twenty-eight times. With this, he wanted to manifest the quantity of the generations from David until Christ who was born from his lineage. Indeed, from David until Christ there are twenty-eight generations, as the evangelist Matthew declared regarding the genealogy of Christ. He said: “From David until the deportation to Babylon fourteen generations” (Mt 1:17). ### Exhortation addressed to the disciple. Ps 135:1-9

29 Pay attention to this, O beloved, so that you may understand that with God there is (no) abrogated word and every word in the books that is repeated has an explanation. Here is the beginning of the interpretation of Psalm 135: “Give thanks to God because he is good, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal”. “Give thanks to the God of gods, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal”. “Give thanks to the God of lords, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:1-3). With these three (thanksgivings) he manifested the Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit, when he says: “Give thanks to God, to the God of gods and to the Lord of Lords”. He also says: “Who, he alone, did great and wonderful things, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:4). “Who made the heavens with his wisdom, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:5). “Who established the earth upon the water, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:6). “Who, he alone, made the great luminaries, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. To the sun he subjected the day, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. To the moon and the stars he subjected the night, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:7-9).

30 He has clarified that the triune Saint does not need others, but he alone is the artificer of wonders and creator of the heavens, of the earth, of the moon, of the stars, a worker of power solely from himself. He also says: “Who killed the Egyptians together with their firstborn, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. He brought Israel out from their midst, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal, with a strong hand and with an outstretched arm, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:10-12). The Egyptians are the army of Pharaoh, just as the demons are the army of the devil whom God the Father struck with the hands of Christ his Son, who is his strong hand and with his outstretched arm with just blows, in his humanization and with his sufferings, until he brought the deceased children of Adam out of Sheol, from their midst. 0. 31 Instead, he freed the living from their slavery through holy baptism. For this reason he says: “Who split the Red Sea in two and divided it, alleluia, 15 because his mercy is eternal, and brought the Israelites out to the opposite shore, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Ps 135:13-14). As for what he said: “Who split the Red Sea in two”; it is a figure of Sheol and it is a figure of baptism, as I explained to you in the book of the interpretation of the Torah regarding what it says: “Who split in two and divided”. This means that he, with a distinct sign in multiple distinctions to the dead and to the living, because for the dead children of Adam he separated Sheol from them with his crucifixion and with his descent into it (Sheol), and from it he made them ascend and freed them from their enemies (1 Pt 4:4-6; Eph 4:9-10; Rev 1:18). To the living, however, he separated holy baptism: they descend into it and ascend from it freed from the impure spirits that reside with them, just as the Israelites freed themselves from the slavery of the Egyptians in their descent into the Red Sea and their ascent from it. his mercy is eternal. Who led his people out into the desert, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. Who made water gush from the dry rock, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Psalm 135:15-16). The desert is the holy church as I manifested to you in the book of the interpretation of the Torah, because when the faithful emerge from baptism after 5. the drowning of the powers of the demons that reside with them, they enter the church. As for the water that gushes for them from the rock, it is the water and the blood that gushes for them from the side of Christ (Jn 19:34), the true rock that they drink in the church so that they may live in it for eternity (1 Cor 10:4).

### Ps 135:17-22: prem
ise and text

10 33 As I explained to you in that book, this the faithful obtained from the time in which the apostles preached to them, because they, through the preaching of the apostles, freed themselves from the slavery to Satan who reigned over them with hardness and force. Therefore David said: “And killed powerful kings, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. And killed great kings, alleluia, because 15 his mercy is eternal. Seon, king of the Amorites, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal and Og king of Basan, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. And gave their land as an inheritance, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. Inheritance to Israel his servant, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Psalm 135:17-22). but God gave them victory over them, they defeated them, destroyed them, passed through and inherited their land (Nm 21:21ff.; Dt 3). These became a type of the demons of the air, the army of the devil who is between heaven (Eph 6:12) and earth who prevents the faithful from transiting to heaven during their life, and after their death. During their life they entice them with the actions of this world 5 and make them lazy from the praise of God and from his sanctification, until they die. For this reason they seize their souls and prevent them from ascending (to heaven) because they have become lazy from praise like them (did), consequently, they deserve to decay together with them, with justice. These are those who dwell inside idols and people worship them. The apostles defeated them and expelled them from the souls of humans and from idols; (the demons and Satan) who reigned over the souls of humans, subjugated them and lorded over them. Eschatological fulfillment of the prophecy of Psalm 135

35 The word of the Psalm has truly ascertained that he gave their land to Israel his servant. They are the apostles his servants who inherited their houses and the souls of the people who reigned over them; the apostles inherited them with the baptism of the Holy Spirit that dwells in the souls of people and expels from them the unclean spirits, princes of the air (Eph 6:12). If they dedicate themselves to praise and sanctification, after their death, he will help them against them and will make them reach Paradise, until the moment in which their number will be completed and they will ascend and inherit their land in truth, the exalted order from which they fell. Therefore the Psalm says: “Because God in our suffering remembered us, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. From the hand of our enemy he freed us, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal

with the Holy Spirit that dwells inside us if we dedicate ourselves to his praise and his sanctification and to the listening of his word, that is, the food of the Spirit. He gave the food to the spirit by the hand of his disciples, through their preaching. They were heard by all peoples because he always gives his food to every flesh. Therefore he completes the profession with the Psalm: “Exalt the God of heaven, 5 alleluia, because his mercy is eternal. Exalt the Lord of lords, alleluia, because his mercy is eternal” (Psalm 135:26). He gives an order to the faithful with rigor so that they may believe in him and exalt him always and dedicate themselves to his praise and his sanctification always for as much as he provides for them. To him be glory for the ages of ages amen. Text of Psalm 150 (LXX) 10 37 “Praise God in his saints, praise in the firmament of his power” (v. 1). “Praise him for his power, praise him for the abundance of his greatness” (v. 2). “Praise him with the sound of the horn, praise him with song and with the lyre” (v. 3). “Praise him with the drum and with joy, praise him with strings and with the harp” (v. 4). “Praise him with cymbals of melodious sound, praise him 15 with high voices and with trills (of joy)” (v. 5). “Every living thing praise the Lord” (v. 6). Hermeneutics of Psalm 150

38 He said “praise him” ten times, evoking the nine angelic orders whose names are known in relation to the praise of God, and to them he has added the human race for praise, because the human race is the tenth order. Indeed, the order to which the faithful ascend rises above all orders, 20 as the Lord said: “Because the last will be first and the first will be last” (Mt 20:16). How delicious is his word for the human race! “Praise him with cymbals and with trills (of joy)”, because this is a praise of victory of the Hebrews and thus all peoples rejoice when they defeat their enemies, because those among the children of Adam who defeat the powers of the enemy

ascend to their order and praise together with the angels. He who among all peoples defeats them ascends to their order. For this reason he concluded his word by saying: “Every living thing praise the Lord” (150:6). To him be glory in the ages of ages, amen. Hermeneutics of Daniel 3 5 39 Hermeneutics of the fourth vision of Daniel and of the canticle of the three young men. Fourth vision of Daniel and canticle of the three young men. In them he manifested the victory of Satan over the children of Adam when he sank them into Sheol, and the descent of the Son of God to them (1 Pt 3:19) and he freed them from it with his power. He established the measure from the time in which Adam was created until the birth of Christ, and the duration of the years that he would live 10 on earth, until he would be crucified, descended to Sheol and would have saved them. All this is evident in the fourth vision of Daniel with a manifest indication. Exhortation to the disciple. Typological hermeneutics: Adam, Satan, Nebuchadnezzar

40 Pay attention to what I declare to you regarding this and glorify God. It is as we said in the interpretation of the Torah that the slavery of the children of Israel became a type of the enslavement of the children of Adam to 15 Satan. For this reason Daniel in this book indicated that Nebuchadnezzar captured the children of Israel because of their rebellion against God and deported them from their holy land to Babylon, his country, and in it he subjugated them, he and his army of Chaldeans, just as Satan captured Adam and his lineage because of their rebellion against God and deported them from the holy Paradise to this world, land of darkness which is his country and the seat of his administration and 20 in it he subjugated them, he and his army. trumpet, of the harp, of the lyre, of the cymbals and with every entertainment, so much so that they prostrated themselves to the golden image that he had raised, in the same way Satan made all the children of Adam wander, through the lust of this transitory world, that is, pleasure and entertainment, so much so that they submitted, served him, submitted themselves to his orders and all perished. Quadruple repetition of “practice of entertainment” 41 For this reason Daniel repeated four times “the practice of entertainment” to declare that Satan made humans wander through pleasure and entertainment, so much so that they submitted to him, since every lust of the world is entertainment and pleasure. As Nebuchadnezzar introduced the three young men into the burning furnace because they had not obeyed him and did not prostrate themselves 10 to his image, so Satan introduced all the righteous who did not obey his order, Abraham, Isaac, Jacob and other righteous, and the prophets, he put them into Sheol because of the transgression of Adam their father. The fire, however, did not burn them as it burned the Chaldeans, the army of Nebuchadnezzar. Indeed, in Sheol, the fire did not burn the righteous, in the same way as those who obeyed Satan, but stood far from the three young men in the burning furnace, but was for 15 them like fresh dew. 42 It is as the holy Gospel says about the parable of the rich man and Lazarus the 20 Poor (Lk 16:19-31). It says that the rich man was in Sheol and Lazarus in the bosom of Abraham; the rich man raised his eyes while he was in pain, saw Abraham from afar and Lazarus in his bosom and asked Abraham to send Lazarus to him so that he might dip his fingers in fresh water and cool his tongue for the heat of the fire that was in it.

### The incarnation of the angel

10 52 His word, forty-nine cubits, clarified that Satan and his army ruled over the world and burns them in the fire of Sheol for 4,900 years, in the same manner as the forty-nine cubits, because to each cubit correspond one hundred years. Indeed, from the time of Adam's transgression until the beginning of the reign of Nebuchadnezzar, there are 4,900 years, as he says in metaphor that the fire inside it (the furnace) rose forty-nine 15 cubits. This means that the fire of the demons was raised to the same measure as that period but did not burn the righteous who were in Sheol, just as it did not burn the three youths, but it burned the apostate Jews, the people of Satan. As he says that it (the fire) burned the Chaldeans, the people of Nebuchadnezzar, while the righteous inside the fire trusted in God and glorified him without it burning them, and it never touched them.” Declaration of the three youths on chronology

62 Therefore, when they finished the thirty-three times, they said: “Glorify him and increase his sublimity forever because he saved us from Sheol and freed us from the hand of death and saved us from the burning furnace, from the midst of the flame that was burning” (Dn 3:88). They ascertained that he saved Adam and his lineage from Sheol in the thirty-third year, therefore he ordered those 20 who believed in him after his crucifixion to give him thanks; they were certain that his mercy for them would last forever and that he would not change it towards them, as he changed (it) towards the people of the law of the Torah. Explanation of the word “blessed” 63 For this reason they called them “blessed”, saying: “Bless God, 25 9 you who worship God, the God of gods!” (Dn 3:90). They ascertained that their worship was blessed and (that) they themselves were also blessed. They glorified and professed and his mercy towards them will last forever. may know and understand that from Adam until the flood there are 2256 years; from the flood until the birth of Abraham 1072; from the birth of Abraham until the exodus of the children of Israel from the land of Egypt there are 507 years and they lived in the desert for forty years. Their number from Adam until the end of their stay in the desert was 3875 years and this figure is known from the book 5 of the Torah.

### B- From the entry into the promised land to Saul

70 God revealed this to him, through a sign. He said to him again: “Take three 15 cows, three rams, three goats, a dove and a turtledove” (Gen 15:9), he ordered him to divide the cows, the rams, the goats and not to divide the birds (Gen 15:10). The number of those he divided was nine and they became eighteen. He did not divide, however, the dove and the turtledove. The sum of their number is twenty. He said to him furthermore: “You will sleep with your fathers and after that they will emigrate to a foreign country for 400 years” (Gen 15:13, 15). With this word he manifested that those twenty (years) through which he indicated were calculated 20 from after the death of Abraham until the birth of Christ, 2000 years; to each year of them correspond 100 years, as we said at the beginning. of Egypt as migrants except for 217 years. However, that sum of 400 years on earth of which he spoke is a reference to the stay of Christ on earth, which he lived for 400 months from his birth to his crucifixion. In the year 33 he was crucified and descended to Sheol and freed all those who were inside it, as the three youths said in their praise.

Indeed Eve transgressed on the day of Friday which is the 15th of the solar month of Nisan, and on the day of Friday which is the 15th of the month of Nisan, in the month of Adar (the angel) announced to the virgin Mary. In the same way, on the lunar day of the night and in the solar month in which Eve became sad and in it Mary rejoiced, and in it Christ was crucified.

Indeed, as he rose without suffering and without a second death, so we will all rise. Indeed, the apostle designated “his birth” as the resurrection of the Lord, because his resurrection by his will is his 10 birth. Indeed, as he came out of the womb of the Virgin on the day of his birth and the seal of his virginity did not change, so, on the day of his resurrection he came out of the tomb still sealed, without change. 1 Homily 12. On the consolation of believers and on their patience in sadness. O beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your heart with the light of the Holy Spirit the consoler so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity. You asked me to explain what is the reason for so much sadness of those 5. who have believed in our Lord Christ and he is capable of making sadness go away from them. I explain to you the reason for this with the help of our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living God (Mt 16:16).

10 2 Pay attention to what I remind you of this, because I explained to you in the book of the explanation of the humanization of the Son of God and of his crucifixion, that the reason for his crucifixion and his passion was because of his justice and his mercy. He became human because of justice and resembled us in every action except sin (Heb 4:15) until he redeemed us from the hand of our enemy, with justice, without injustice. In the 15 same way, know that the reason for so much sadness in believers is still because of his justice.

### Condition of humans before the coming of Christ

3 It is factual that before the coming of our Lord Christ paradise was closed because of the transgression of Adam (Gen 3:24) and every person became mortal, both the sinner and the righteous, descended into Sheol because of Adam his father (Rom 5:14), as I explained to you in the book of the explanation of his humanization and his crucifixion. Thus, Adam died and stayed in Sheol 5 4,600 years and Noah the righteous, without stain in his generation (Gen 6:9), stayed in Sheol for the duration of 3,000 years. And Abraham, head of the patriarchs who was called “friend of God” (Is 41:8; Jas 2:23), stayed in Sheol for the duration of 2,000 years. And Moses who spoke with God (exchanging) 570 words, in the same manner as a dialogue between companions, stayed in Sheol for the duration of 1,700 years. 10 Merits of the humanization of the Son of God. Fatigue and sadness: they are the toll to access the merits of Christ 4 Such was the situation until our Lord Christ, Son of God became human and redeemed them with his soul from the transgression of Adam their father and paid on their behalf the eternal damnation they deserved. He died for them and descended to them and made them all rise from death and from Sheol to the 15 paradise of joy. And to all those who believed in him, those who observe his commandments and would be baptized in his name he promised that they would not see Sheol and would not descend to it, but that in the moment in which their soul would separate from their body, they would ascend to paradise and would rejoice in it, with Adam, Noah, Abraham and Moses. If he had granted this joy to the believers without fatigue, nor sadness, he would have 20 done wrong to Adam, Noah, Abraham and Moses. Indeed, each of them did not receive that joy, if not after the fatigue in Sheol during that long stay.

Paraphrase of the Sermon on the Mount (Mt 5-7) 5 For this reason, for those who believed in him, he arranged in this world a strict law and ordered them saying: “Enter through the narrow gate, because the spacious gate and the wide road is that which leads to perdition and there are many who enter into it. But the narrow gate and the rough way is that which leads to life and there are few who find it” (Mt 7:13- 5. 14). He also says: “Blessed are those who hunger and thirst for justice because they will be satisfied” (Mt 5:6). “Blessed are those who in this world are in mourning because they will rejoice” (Mt 5:4). “Blessed are those who weep because they will laugh” (Mt 5:4). “Blessed are the poor in spirit because theirs is the Kingdom of Heaven” (Mt 5:3). The practice of asceticism as a response to salvation 10 6 He commanded them hunger and thirst, sadness, weeping, and poverty in this world, so that they would choose this for themselves and practice it voluntarily. He arranged for them continuous prayer and fasting and that they would diminish animal pleasure. He arranged for all of them the fasting of Wednesday and Friday, every week, always and the annual fasting of forty days 15 Let them fast according to their capacity from food and drink, and in it (in Lent) let them fast from animal pleasure. The order to hate wealth and to cultivate forgiveness

7 He commanded them to hate wealth, to renounce it, and to give it to the poor for the sake of their souls, and to keep only what would be sufficient for their day. For they do not know if they will be alive until the following day. He commanded them to forgive their neighbor when they commit faults against them and to renounce vengeance, in order to bear with one another.

8 It is sufficient for them to marry only one woman in this world; let them endure every action of hers, except for the sin of adultery (Mt 5:32; 19:9). Let a man not be voluptuous, nor a lover of the pleasure of this world; instead, let him resist this greatly and distance himself from it (pleasure) as much as he is able. 9 By taking on this labor and enduring it voluntarily, it will become like the long labor that Adam, Noah, Abraham, and Moses endured in Sheol for many years. He has caused the faithful, with this little labor, to receive what those received with long labor. The labor of the former is comparable to one who works all day, while the labor of the latter is like that of one who worked for an hour and received the same reward as those who labored the entire day.

15 10 As it says in the holy Gospel: “The Kingdom of Heaven is like a landowner of a vineyard. He went out in the morning to hire laborers for his vineyard. He agreed with them for a denarius a day for each. And again, he went out at the third hour and found others (laborers) unemployed and sent them to his vineyard without agreeing (on the wage) with them. And again, he went out at the sixth hour and found others (laborers) 20 unemployed and sent them likewise to his vineyard. And again, he went out at the ninth hour and sent others to his vineyard. And again, he went out at the eleventh hour and found others, and said to them: ‘Why do you stand here all day idle?’”.

### Hermeneutics of the Gospel pericope
From Adam to Moses
ከሠተ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረሰዮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ አምኑ ፣ ቦቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ዕሩያን" ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ጻመው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ብዙኃ ። እስመ ፡ ወይ ንሰ ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ገቢረ ፣ ሥምረቱ ፡ ወጻማ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ። ወዕሴተ" ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡፣ ገነተ'' ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘአስተዳለዎ፤' ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለዘርኡ ። እስመ ፣ አዳም ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘተገበረ ፡ እምጽባሕ ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘኖኀ ፡ ጻጸማሁ ፣ በውስተ'' ፡ ሲኦል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ነዊኅ'' ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ። ጣከመ ፣ ጻማሆሙ ፡ ለእለ" ፡ ተገበሩ'' ፡ እም ጽባሕ ፡ እስከ ፡፣ ምሴት ። ወኖኅ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ እለ" ፡ ተገበሩ ፡፣ በ፻ሰዓት ፣ እምዕለት'' ። ወአብርሃም ፡ መጽአ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡፣ በከመ ፣ እለ" ፣ ተገበሩ ፡ በ፪ሰዓት ፡ እመዓልት” ። ወሙሴ ፡ መጽአ ፡
12 With this word he manifested that to those who have believed in him through a meager labor, in the Paradise of delight, he has made them equal to those who have suffered in Sheol for many years. Indeed, the meaning of life is the fulfilling of his will and (enduring) the tribulation for his name. The reward signifies the Paradise of delight that (God) prepared for Adam and his lineage. Adam, in fact, was created first for this, and it is he who worked from the morning; he is the one for whom his tribulation in Sheol was prolonged for a long time on account of the long days he lived in it, like the labor of those who worked from morning until evening. And Noah came after him like those who worked at the third hour of the day. And Abraham came after him like those who came at the sixth hour of the day. And Moses came after him like those who worked at the ninth hour of the day. From Moses to Christ

15 13 And those who believed in Christ came after these, like those who worked at the eleventh hour of the day. Therefore they said to him: “As for us, we have not found anyone for whom to work”, because those who believed in Christ were peoples who worshipped idols. Neither prophet nor apostles were ever sent to them before his holy disciples. They are those who 20 have suffered in this world by taking on his difficult commandments, and have obtained as much as those obtained in the Paradise of delight, without descending into Sheol, nor suffering within it. Indeed, for their toil of one hour, they received the reward before those had received it. Just as the thief on the right (of Christ) obtained it, who believed in him and preceded everyone in the Paradise of delight and received the reward 25 before them.

### Concluding reflection of the parable
ወኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ይኔጽርዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወይደመሩ፤ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ ወይትፌሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወይሳተፉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ኖ በሥጋሁ ፡፣ ወደሙ ። ወአሐዱሰ ፡ እምእሉንቱ ፡ ኢረከበ' ፡ ሃ 16[;፤. ውእቱ' ፡ በሕይወቱ" ፡ በዝዓለም ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብፁዓት ፡ አፅይንቲክሙ ፡ 'እለ ፡ ርእያ ። እብለክሙ' ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወጻድቃን ፡ ወነገሥት ፡ ፈተው ፣፡ ይርአዩ ፡ ዘርኢክሙ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወኢርእዩ ፡ ‹ወይስምዑ ፡ ዘሰማዕክሙ ፡ ወኢሰምዑ ፡ እስመ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ፅሴተ ፡ መንግሥተ" ፡ ሰማያት ፣ በዝዓለም ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ሞት ፡ እስመ ፡ እለ ፡ በመንግሥተ'' ፡ ሰማያት ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ'!' ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘይሬእይዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይሳተፉ ፣፡
14 All the faithful look to Christ in this world, before death, and are united to him, rejoice in him, and participate with him in his body and his blood (Heb 2:14). Not one of those obtained this during their life in this world. This God says in the holy Gospel to those who believed in him: “Blessed are your eyes that have seen, I say to you, because many 5 prophets, righteous men, and kings wanted to see what you have seen and did not see, and to hear what you have heard but did not hear” (Mt 13:16-17). Indeed, the faithful have received the reward of the Kingdom of Heaven in this world, before death, since those who are in the Kingdom of Heaven have no (other) joy except to see Christ and to participate together with him. 10

15 The faithful in this world see him and participate in this world in his body and his blood. As for those who are in Paradise, they did not obtain this in their life, and until now they have not ascended to the Kingdom to receive it, because he has left them in Paradise until the number of those who 15 believe in him is completed and they ascend and obtain the eternal kingdom forever. He has indeed left them in Paradise so that they would not obtain the reward before those who had believed in him, as he says in his parable that he gave the reward to the last before the first. He gave them that in this world, before those, and again he will give it to them before them in his resurrection. Paranesis addressed to the disciple 20

16 O beloved! Observe this great grace that he has given to the faithful for the little labor that they endure in this world (Rom 8:18). If a person among us were to live in this world for one hundred years enduring the labor of the 25 law of Christ, his labor will not be like (that of) one hour in Sheol, among the labor of those who endured it for many years. He made this measure small, and this labor in this world small, so that through them they might obtain what those obtained through a long labor and after thousands of years. But we groan for the labor (Rom 8:23) and we do not remember his word that says: “He who will have patience to the end, will be saved” (Mt 24:13), and 30

18 And our Lord, to him be glory!, knew that our laziness was great, due to our love for the goods of this world, and if we enjoy the pleasure of this world and rejoice in it, the eternal grace will depart from us in this 10 world. For this reason, he has multiplied upon us the affliction of this world and its sadness, so that because of a meager labor we obtain the eternal kingdom. We cannot indeed receive without labor. Adam, Noah, Abraham, and Moses will be as if (God) had acted without justice with them, because they obtained it with labor, while we obtain it with rest. For this reason, he multiplied upon us the sadness and the affliction 15 of enemies. Gospel texts on the sacrifices required of the faithful 19 And he said: “Behold I send you as lambs in the midst of wolves, they will raise their hand against you and will persecute you, they will lead you to judges and to chains, to kings and to governors because of my name, and it will be a testimony, and do not think about what you will say in response; I will give you mouth and wisdom from the Holy Spirit through which you will speak and they will not be able to reply to you” (Mt 10:16a, 18, 19, 20). He also said: “Blessed are those who are persecuted because of righteousness because theirs is the Kingdom of Heaven” (Mt 5:10). He also said: “He who wants to save his soul, let him abandon it, and he who will have abandoned his soul because of me, will find it. What advantage will a man have if he gains the whole world and loses his soul?” (Mt 16:25-26). He also said: “He who loves his soul, let him 10 abandon it, and he who in this world will have abandoned his soul, he will preserve it for eternal life” (Jn 12:25). And he said: “Whoever wants to come to me, let him abandon his soul, carry the cross of his death, and follow me” (Mt 8:34).

15 21 And he said: “You will have tribulation in the world, but take courage because I have conquered the world” (Jn 16:33). And he said: “You will be hated by all because of my name, but he who will prolong his patience, he will be saved” (Mt 10:22). And a hair of your head will not be lost: with your patience you will possess your souls” (Lk 21:18-19).

### Authority of the words of Jesus

22 And this he said to them not as a prophet says it regarding what will 20 happen to them, but rather his word is powerful because he is God, as he said to the heaven and to the earth: “Exist!” and they existed. He created everything with his word: “Exist!” and it came into existence. The same will happen for what he said: “You will be hated by all”. “You will have tribulations in this world”. With such words he created tribulations upon us and made us hated by all so that with our patience in this we might obtain the eternal life that no one can 25 receive without labor.

### Again on the purpose of tribulations

23 (God) arranged this for us because he knows that we become lazy and abandon the labor that he commanded us to endure voluntarily, in hunger and thirst, in mourning and weeping, and we lose the joy in this world. He forgave us, to him be glory!, and brought upon us tribulation and hatred from people, so that if we endure this, we obtain the eternal joy. If we observe 5 what he has commanded us in terms of hunger and thirst, of mourning and weeping, we would not be subjected to the trial deriving from the tribulation of enemies, nor from any hostility of men. If, however, (God) sees that we love pleasure and bodily relaxation, he will make torment prevail over us without our will, to correct us through it, and enduring it will be our remuneration. 10 24 If he did not love us, he would not have corrected us, because a father does not correct anyone except his own son, while he does not correct a stranger. As the apostle Paul says: “Do not consider the punishment of God hard, nor must you draw back when he corrects you, because God corrects whom he loves” (Heb 12:6-7). He will judge all the children he loves. As for someone among us who desires that 15 tribulation from the enemy not happen to him, nor affliction, nor illness, nor the death of those he loves, let him observe his commandment and labor always, that is, (endure) hunger, thirst, mourning, and weeping. 1 destruction of his goods, or through the rising (aggression) of his : enemy. Let him know that God has loved him and for this reason has corrected him. He must labor . of his own will always so that he may be saved from the tribulation that strikes him . without his will. As for him who does not fulfill what (God) has 1 commanded him always, in terms of hunger, of thirst, of mourning, and of weeping, he will not have ® illness, nor death; his loved one will not be struck by tribulation from the a enemies, nor by the destruction of his goods. Know this: that God hates him and . Therefore he has left him in this world without condemnation, until he condemns him ᾿ after his death with eternal condemnation. The call of the parable of the insensitive rich man and the poor Lazarus 26 As it says in the holy Gospel about the rich man who enjoyed himself in this world 1 that after his death they put him in the condemnation of fire. When he asked Abraham to send him Lazarus the poor to refresh his tongue with a little water with the tip of his fingers, Abraham said to him: “Remember that you received your goods in your life in the world while Lazarus was in torment. He in this place rests while you are condemned” (Lk 16:19-25). Lessons of the Gospel parable 15 27 With this word, the holy Gospel has manifested to us that he who does not weary himself in this world with the service of the commandment, God will not bring upon him the tribulation as he brought labor, poverty, and illness upon Lazarus, because this will happen after his death in the eternal condemnation. Instead, he who wearies himself in this world, God puts him to the test: he will rest after death in the eternal rest. We must not therefore consider the trial of this world hard when we are put to the test, but let us welcome this with gratitude and joy, so that we know that in this there is the benefit that leads us to the eternal joy. Final supplication and doxology
